                        page-break-after: always;
                        padding: 2px;
                    }

                    .product-title {
                        font-size: 10px;
                        font-weight: 600;
                        margin-bottom: 2px;
                        white-space: nowrap;
                        overflow: hidden;
                        text-overflow: ellipsis;
                        width: 100%;
                    }

                    .barcode-svg svg {
                        max-width: 100%;
                        height: auto;
                    }

                    .product-cost {
                        font-size: 11px;
                        font-weight: 700;
                        margin-top: 1px;
                    }
                </style>
            </head>
            <body>
                <div class="${paperSize === 'single-label' ? 'barcode-sheet-single' : 'barcode-sheet-grid'}">
                    ${labelsHtml}
                </div>
                <script>
                    window.onload = function() {
                        window.print();
                        setTimeout(function() { window.close(); }, 500);
                    };
                </script>
            </body>
            </html>
        `);
        printWindow.document.close();
    };

    const fetchProducts = async () => {
        try {
            const params: any = {};
            if (search) params.q = search;
            if (categoryFilter) params.categoryId = categoryFilter;
            const res = await axios.get('/api/products/search', { params });
            setProducts(res.data);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(err);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        axios.get('/api/categories').then((r) => setCategories(r.data));
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        const timer = setTimeout(fetchProducts, 300);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }, [search, categoryFilter]);

    useEffect(() => {
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }, [search, categoryFilter]);

    const handleDelete = async (product: Product) => {
        const result = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Delete Product?',
            text: `Are you sure you want to delete "${product.name}"?`,
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonColor: '#ff6b6b',
            cancelButtonColor: '#555',
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes, delete it',
            background: '#1a1a2e',
            color: '#eee',
        });

        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            try {
                await axios.delete(`/api/products/${product.id}`);
                setProducts((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== product.id));
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: 'Deleted!',
                    text: 'Product has been deleted.',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
                if (viewingProduct?.id === product.id) {
                    setViewingProduct(null);
                }
            } catch (err: any) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Error',
                    text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to delete product',
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
            }
        }
    };

    const toggleSort = (field: string) => {
        if (sortBy === field) {
            setSortOrder(prev => prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
        } else {
            setSortBy(field);
            setSortOrder('asc');
        }
        setCurrentPage(1);
    };

    const renderSortIndicator = (field: string) => {
        if (sortBy !== field) {
            return <ArrowUpDown size={12} className="sort-icon" style={{ marginLeft: '4px', opacity: 0.4 }} />;
        }
        return sortOrder === 'asc' ? 
            <ChevronUp size={12} className="sort-icon" style={{ marginLeft: '4px', color: 'var(--accent)' }} /> : 
            <ChevronDown size={12} className="sort-icon" style={{ marginLeft: '4px', color: 'var(--accent)' }} />;
    };

    const handleOpenViewModal = async (product: Product) => {
        setViewingProduct(product);
        try {
            const res = await axios.get(`/api/products/${product.id}/barcode`);
            setViewingProductBarcodeUrl(res.data.barcodeImageUrl);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Failed to load barcode URL", err);
            setViewingProductBarcodeUrl(null);
        }
    };

    const getSortedProducts = () => {
        return [...products].sort((a, b) => {
            let valA: any = a[sortBy as keyof Product];
            let valB: any = b[sortBy as keyof Product];

            if (sortBy === 'category') {
                valA = a.category?.name || '';
                valB = b.category?.name || '';
            }

            if (typeof valA === 'string' && typeof valB === 'string') {
                const cmp = valA.localeCompare(valB, undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' });
                return sortOrder === 'asc' ? cmp : -cmp;
            }

            if (typeof valA === 'number' && typeof valB === 'number') {
                return sortOrder === 'asc' ? valA - valB : valB - valA;
            }

            // Fallback for dates/others
            if (valA < valB) return sortOrder === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
            if (valA > valB) return sortOrder === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
            return 0;
        });
    };

    const sortedProducts = getSortedProducts();
    const itemsPerPage = 50;
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(sortedProducts.length / itemsPerPage) || 1;
    const paginatedProducts = sortedProducts.slice(
        (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage,
        currentPage * itemsPerPage
    );

    const renderPagination = () => {
        if (totalPages <= 1) return null;

        const pages = [];
        const maxVisiblePages = 5;
        let startPage = Math.max(1, currentPage - Math.floor(maxVisiblePages / 2));
        let endPage = Math.min(totalPages, startPage + maxVisiblePages - 1);

        if (endPage - startPage + 1 < maxVisiblePages) {
            startPage = Math.max(1, endPage - maxVisiblePages + 1);
        }

        for (let i = startPage; i <= endPage; i++) {
            pages.push(i);
        }

        return (
            <div className="pagination-container">
                <div className="pagination-info">
                    Showing <strong>{sortedProducts.length === 0 ? 0 : (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage + 1}</strong> - <strong>{Math.min(currentPage * itemsPerPage, sortedProducts.length)}</strong> of <strong>{sortedProducts.length}</strong> products
                </div>
                <div className="pagination-controls">
                    <button
                        className="pagination-btn"
                        disabled={currentPage === 1}
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage(1)}
                        title="First Page"
                    >
                        <ChevronsLeft size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        className="pagination-btn"
                        disabled={currentPage === 1}
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => prev - 1)}
                        title="Previous Page"
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft size={16} />
                    </button>

                    {startPage > 1 && <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', margin: '0 4px' }}>...</span>}

                    {pages.map((p) => (
                        <button
                            key={p}
                            className={`pagination-btn ${currentPage === p ? 'active' : ''}`}
                            onClick={() => setCurrentPage(p)}
                        >
                            {p}
                        </button>
                    ))}

                    {endPage < totalPages && <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', margin: '0 4px' }}>...</span>}

                    <button
                        className="pagination-btn"
                        disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => prev + 1)}
                        title="Next Page"
                    >
                        <ChevronRight size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        className="pagination-btn"
                        disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                        onClick={() => setCurrentPage(totalPages)}
                        title="Last Page"
                    >
                        <ChevronsRight size={16} />
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    };

    const columns: Record<string, { label: string; header: React.ReactNode; cell: (p: Product) => React.ReactNode }> = {
        image: {
            label: 'Image',
            header: <span style={{ userSelect: 'none' }}>Image</span>,
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                >
                    {product.imageUrl ? (
                        <img src={product.imageUrl} alt={product.name} className="table-product-thumb" />
                    ) : (
                        <div className="table-product-placeholder">
                            <Package size={18} />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        barcode: {
            label: 'Barcode/SKU',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('barcode'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Barcode/SKU {renderSortIndicator('barcode')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                    style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: '0.85rem', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}
                >
                    {product.barcode}
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        name: {
            label: 'Product Name',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('name'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Product Name {renderSortIndicator('name')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                    style={{ fontWeight: '600' }}
                >
                    <span>{product.name}</span>
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        category: {
            label: 'Category',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('category'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Category {renderSortIndicator('category')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                >
                    <span className="product-category">{product.category.name}</span>
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        costPrice: {
            label: 'Cost Price',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('costPrice'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Cost Price {renderSortIndicator('costPrice')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                    style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}
                >
                    ₱{product.costPrice ? product.costPrice.toFixed(2) : '0.00'}
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        retailPrice: {
            label: 'Retail Price',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('retailPrice'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Retail Price {renderSortIndicator('retailPrice')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                    className="text-green"
                    style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}
                >
                    ₱{product.retailPrice > 0 ? product.retailPrice.toFixed(2) : product.price.toFixed(2)}
                </motion.td>
            )
        },
        stockQty: {
            label: 'Stock Quantity',
            header: (
                <div className="th-content" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSort('stockQty'); }} style={{ userSelect: 'none', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    Stock Quantity {renderSortIndicator('stockQty')}
                </div>
            ),
            cell: (product) => (
                <motion.td
                    layout
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                >
                    <span className={`stock-badge ${
                        product.stockQty === 0 
                            ? 'stock-out' 
                            : product.stockQty <= 10 
                                ? 'stock-low' 
                                : 'stock-good'
                    }`}>
                        {product.stockQty === 0 
                            ? 'Out of Stock' 
                            : product.stockQty <= 10 
                                ? `Low Stock (${product.stockQty})` 
                                : `${product.stockQty} in stock`
                        }
                    </span>
                </motion.td>
            )
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="page">
            <div className="page-header">
                <div>
                    <h1>Inventory</h1>
                    <p>{products.length} products total</p>
                </div>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <input
                        type="file"
                        ref={fileInputRef}
                        onChange={handleImportExcel}
                        accept=".xlsx, .xls"
                        style={{ display: 'none' }}
                    />
                    <motion.button
                        className="btn btn-secondary"
                        onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                        whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
                    >
                        <Upload size={18} />
                        Import Excel
                    </motion.button>
                    <motion.button
                        className="btn btn-secondary"
                        onClick={handleExportExcel}
                        whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
                    >
                        <Download size={18} />
                        Export Excel
                    </motion.button>
                    <motion.button
                        className="btn btn-primary"
                        onClick={() => navigate('/products/new')}
                        whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
                    >
                        <Plus size={18} />
                        Add Product
                    </motion.button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="filters-bar">
                <div className="search-box">
                    <Search size={18} />
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Search products, barcodes..."
                        value={search}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    />
                </div>
                <div className="filter-select">
                    <Filter size={18} />
                    <select value={categoryFilter} onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}>
                        <option value="">All Categories</option>
                        {categories.map((c) => (
                            <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div className="filter-select">
                    <ArrowUpDown size={18} />
                    <select value={sortBy} onChange={(e) => setSortBy(e.target.value)}>
                        <option value="name">Sort by Name</option>
                        <option value="barcode">Sort by Barcode/SKU</option>
                        <option value="price">Sort by Price</option>
                        <option value="stockQty">Sort by Stock Qty</option>
                        <option value="category">Sort by Category</option>
                        <option value="createdAt">Sort by Date Added</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div className="filter-select">
                    <select value={sortOrder} onChange={(e) => setSortOrder(e.target.value as 'asc' | 'desc')}>
                        <option value="asc">Ascending</option>
                        <option value="desc">Descending</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
                <div className="loading-container"><div className="spinner" /></div>
            ) : products.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="empty-state">
                    <Package size={48} />
                    <h3>No products found</h3>
                    <p>Add your first product to get started</p>
                </div>
            ) : (
                <motion.div 
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 15 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                >
                    <div className="card" style={{ padding: 0, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                        <div className="table-container">
                            <table>
                                <thead>
                                    <tr>
                                        {columnOrder.map((colKey, index) => (
                                            <motion.th
                                                key={colKey}
                                                layout
                                                transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                                                draggable="true"
                                                onDragStartCapture={(e) => handleDragStart(e, index)}
                                                onDragOverCapture={(e) => handleDragOver(e)}
                                                onDropCapture={(e) => handleDrop(e, index)}
                                                style={{ cursor: 'grab' }}
                                                className={colKey !== 'image' ? 'sortable-th' : ''}
                                            >
                                                {columns[colKey].header}
                                            </motion.th>
                                        ))}
                                        <motion.th
                                            layout
                                            transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                                            style={{ width: '150px', textAlign: 'right' }}
                                        >
                                            Actions
                                        </motion.th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
                                    {paginatedProducts.map((product) => (
                                        <tr
                                            key={product.id}
                                            onClick={() => handleOpenViewModal(product)}
                                            style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                                            className="inventory-table-row"
                                        >
                                            {columnOrder.map((colKey) => (
                                                <Fragment key={colKey}>
                                                    {columns[colKey].cell(product)}
                                                </Fragment>
                                            ))}
                                            <motion.td
                                                layout
                                                transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                                                style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}
                                            >
                                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                                                    <button
                                                        className="btn-icon btn-view"
                                                        title="View Details"
                                                        onClick={(e) => {
                                                            e.stopPropagation();
                                                            handleOpenViewModal(product);
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <Eye size={16} />
                                                    </button>
                                                    <button
                                                        className="btn-icon btn-edit"
                                                        title="Edit Product"
                                                        onClick={(e) => {
                                                            e.stopPropagation();
                                                            navigate(`/products/${product.id}/edit`);
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <Edit3 size={16} />
                                                    </button>
                                                    <button
                                                        className="btn-icon btn-delete"
                                                        title="Delete Product"
                                                        onClick={(e) => {
                                                            e.stopPropagation();
                                                            handleDelete(product);
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <Trash2 size={16} />
                                                    </button>
                                                </div>
                                            </motion.td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))}
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    {renderPagination()}
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* Custom Detail View Modal */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {viewingProduct && (
                    <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={() => setViewingProduct(null)}>
                        <motion.div
                            className="modal-container"
                            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 20 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 20 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                        >
                            <button className="modal-close-btn" onClick={() => setViewingProduct(null)}>
                                <X size={18} />
                            </button>
                            <div className="modal-body">
                                <div className="product-detail-grid">
                                    <div className="product-detail-image-section">
                                        {viewingProduct.imageUrl ? (
                                            <img src={viewingProduct.imageUrl} alt={viewingProduct.name} className="product-detail-image" />
                                        ) : (
                                            <div className="product-detail-image-placeholder">
                                                <Package size={48} />
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                        <div className="product-detail-barcode-area">
                                            {viewingProductBarcodeUrl ? (
                                                <img src={viewingProductBarcodeUrl} alt={viewingProduct.barcode} style={{ maxWidth: '100%', height: 'auto' }} />
                                            ) : (
                                                <div style={{ color: '#000', fontSize: '0.8rem', fontFamily: 'monospace', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                                                    <Barcode size={32} style={{ margin: '0 auto 4px', opacity: 0.7 }} />
                                                    {viewingProduct.barcode}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="product-detail-info-section">
                                        <div className="product-detail-header">
                                            <h2>{viewingProduct.name}</h2>
                                            <span className="product-detail-category-badge">{viewingProduct.category.name}</span>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="product-detail-meta-grid" style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(2, 1fr)', gap: '10px', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                            <div className="product-detail-meta-card">
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-label">Cost Price</div>
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-value" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>₱{viewingProduct.costPrice ? viewingProduct.costPrice.toFixed(2) : '0.00'}</div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="product-detail-meta-card">
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-label">Retail Price</div>
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-value text-green">₱{viewingProduct.retailPrice ? viewingProduct.retailPrice.toFixed(2) : viewingProduct.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="product-detail-meta-card">
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-label">VAT</div>
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-value" style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem' }}>
                                                    {viewingProduct.taxRate}% ({viewingProduct.taxInclusive ? 'Inclusive' : 'Exclusive'})
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="product-detail-meta-card">
                                                <div className="product-detail-meta-label">Stock Status</div>
                                                <div className={`product-detail-meta-value ${
                                                    viewingProduct.stockQty === 0 
                                                        ? 'text-red' 
                                                        : viewingProduct.stockQty <= 10 
                                                            ? 'text-yellow' 
                                                            : 'text-green'
                                                }`}>
                                                    {viewingProduct.stockQty} Units
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="product-detail-description" style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                            <h3>Description</h3>
                                            <p>{viewingProduct.description || 'No description provided.'}</p>
                                        </div>
                                        
                                        <div className="product-detail-stocks" style={{ marginBottom: '16px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', paddingTop: '12px' }}>
                                            {(() => {
                                                const totalCount = viewingProduct.stockItems ? viewingProduct.stockItems.length : 0;
                                                const availCount = viewingProduct.stockItems ? viewingProduct.stockItems.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length : 0;
                                                const soldCount = totalCount - availCount;
                                                
                                                const displayedItems = viewingProduct.stockItems 
                                                    ? viewingProduct.stockItems.filter((s: any) => s.status === activeStockTab) 
                                                    : [];
                                                    
                                                return (
                                                    <>
                                                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '0.95rem', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                                                            Stock Items (S/Ns) — <span className="text-green" style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}>{availCount} Available</span> / {totalCount} Total
                                                        </h3>
                                                        
                                                        <div className="tabs mb-4" style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                                                            <button 
                                                                className={`btn ${activeStockTab === 'AVAILABLE' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}`}
                                                                onClick={() => setActiveStockTab('AVAILABLE')}
                                                                style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: '0.8rem' }}
                                                            >
                                                                Available ({availCount})
                                                            </button>
                                                            <button 
                                                                className={`btn ${activeStockTab === 'SOLD' ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-secondary'}`}
                                                                onClick={() => setActiveStockTab('SOLD')}
                                                                style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: '0.8rem' }}
                                                            >
                                                                Sold ({soldCount})
                                                            </button>
                                                        </div>

                                                        {displayedItems.length > 0 ? (
                                                            <div style={{ border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: '6px', overflow: 'hidden', maxHeight: '200px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
                                                                <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '0.8rem' }}>
                                                                    <thead style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 0, zIndex: 1 }}>
                                                                        <tr style={{ background: 'var(--bg-primary)', textAlign: 'left' }}>
                                                                            <th style={{ padding: '6px 12px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>Serial Number (S/N)</th>
                                                                            <th style={{ padding: '6px 12px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)', textAlign: 'right' }}>Status</th>
                                                                        </tr>
                                                                    </thead>
                                                                    <tbody>
                                                                        {displayedItems.map((s, idx) => (
                                                                            <tr key={s.id} style={{ borderBottom: idx === displayedItems.length - 1 ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--border)', background: idx % 2 === 0 ? 'var(--bg-secondary)' : 'var(--bg-primary)' }}>
                                                                                <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontFamily: 'monospace', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '500' }}>{s.serialNumber}</td>
                                                                                <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                                                                                    <span className={`stock-badge ${s.status === 'AVAILABLE' ? 'stock-good' : 'stock-out'}`} style={{ padding: '4px 8px', fontSize: '0.7rem', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
                                                                                        {s.status}
                                                                                    </span>
                                                                                </td>
                                                                            </tr>
                                                                        ))}
                                                                    </tbody>
                                                                </table>
                                                            </div>
                                                        ) : (
                                                            <p style={{ fontSize: '0.85rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>No {activeStockTab.toLowerCase()} serial numbers found.</p>
                                                        )}
                                                    </>
                                                );
                                            })()}
                                        </div>

                                        <div style={{ marginTop: 'auto', paddingTop: '12px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', fontSize: '0.8rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                                            Date Added: {new Date(viewingProduct.createdAt).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { dateStyle: 'long' })}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="product-detail-footer">
                                <button className="btn btn-secondary" onClick={() => handlePrintBarcodeModal(viewingProduct)}>
                                    <Printer size={16} /> Print Barcode Label
                                </button>
                                <button className="btn btn-secondary" onClick={() => {
                                    setViewingProduct(null);
                                    setActiveStockTab('AVAILABLE');
                                    navigate(`/products/${viewingProduct.id}/edit`);
                                }}>
                                    <Edit3 size={16} /> Edit Details
                                </button>
                                <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => {
                                    setViewingProduct(null);
                                    setActiveStockTab('AVAILABLE');
                                }}>
                                    Close
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </motion.div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    );
}


================================================================================
FILE: client\src\pages\Login.tsx
================================================================================

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { LogIn, Mail, Lock, Zap, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { useBranding } from '../context/BrandingContext';

export default function Login() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    const { login } = useAuth();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { name, subtitle, logoUrl, emailDomain } = useBranding();

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setLoading(true);
        try {
            await login(email, password);
            await Swal.fire({
                icon: 'success',
                title: 'Welcome!',
                text: 'Login successful',
                timer: 1500,
                showConfirmButton: false,
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            });
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: any) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Login Failed',
                text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Invalid credentials',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            });
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="login-page">
            <div className="login-bg-effects">
                <div className="orb orb-1" />
                <div className="orb orb-2" />
                <div className="orb orb-3" />
            </div>
            <motion.div
                className="login-card"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 40, scale: 0.95 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: 'easeOut' }}
            >
                <div className="login-header">
                    {logoUrl ? (
                        <motion.img
                            src={logoUrl}
                            alt="Logo"
                            style={{ width: '80px', height: '80px', borderRadius: '12px', objectFit: 'cover', border: '1px solid var(--border)', marginBottom: '16px' }}
                            initial={{ scale: 0.8 }}
                            animate={{ scale: 1 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
                        />
                    ) : (
                        <motion.div
                            className="login-logo"
                            animate={{ rotate: [0, 10, -10, 0] }}
                            transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 4, ease: 'easeInOut' }}
                        >
                            <Zap size={40} />
                        </motion.div>
                    )}
                    <h1>{name}</h1>
                    <p>{subtitle}</p>
                </div>

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="login-form">
                    <div className="input-group">
                        <Mail size={18} className="input-icon" />
                        <input
                            type="email"
                            placeholder="Email address"
                            value={email}
                            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                            required
                            autoFocus
                        />
                    </div>
                    <div className="input-group">
                        <Lock size={18} className="input-icon" />
                        <input
                            type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                            style={{ paddingRight: '42px' }}
                            placeholder="Password"
                            value={password}
                            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                            required
                        />
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                            style={{
                                position: 'absolute',
                                right: '14px',
                                background: 'none',
                                border: 'none',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                color: 'var(--text-muted)',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                padding: 0,
                                outline: 'none',
                                transition: 'color 0.2s ease',
                            }}
                            onMouseEnter={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)'}
                            onMouseLeave={(e) => e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)'}
                        >
                            {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                    <motion.button
                        type="submit"
                        className="login-btn"
                        disabled={loading}
                        whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                    >
                        {loading ? (
                            <div className="spinner-small" />
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                <LogIn size={18} />
                                Sign In
                            </>
                        )}
                    </motion.button>
                </form>

                <div className="login-footer">
                    <p>Demo: admin@{emailDomain || 'shadow.com'} / admin123</p>
                </div>
            </motion.div>
        </div>
    );
}


================================================================================
FILE: client\src\pages\PaymentModes.tsx
================================================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import axios from 'axios';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { Plus, Edit3, Trash2, CreditCard, Search, X, Check, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

interface PaymentMode {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    description: string;
    isActive: boolean;
    createdAt: string;
}

export default function PaymentModes() {
    const [modes, setModes] = useState<PaymentMode[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
    const { user } = useAuth();

    // Modal / Drawer state
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const [editingMode, setEditingMode] = useState<PaymentMode | null>(null);
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
    const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');

    const fetchModes = async () => {
        try {
            const res = await axios.get('/api/payment-modes');
            setModes(res.data);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('Failed to fetch payment modes:', err);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchModes();
    }, []);

    const openCreateModal = () => {
        setEditingMode(null);
        setName('');
        setDescription('');
        setIsActive(true);
        setError('');
        setIsOpen(true);
    };

    const openEditModal = (mode: PaymentMode) => {
        setEditingMode(mode);
        setName(mode.name);
        setDescription(mode.description);
        setIsActive(mode.isActive);
        setError('');
        setIsOpen(true);
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError('');

        if (!name.trim()) {
            setError('Name is required.');
            return;
        }

        try {
            if (editingMode) {
                // Update
                await axios.put(`/api/payment-modes/${editingMode.id}`, {
                    name,
                    description,
                    isActive,
                });
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: 'Updated!',
                    text: 'Payment mode updated successfully.',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
            } else {
                // Create
                await axios.post('/api/payment-modes', {
                    name,
                    description,
                    isActive,
                });
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: 'Created!',
                    text: 'Payment mode created successfully.',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
            }
            setIsOpen(false);
            fetchModes();
        } catch (err: any) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to save payment mode.');
        }
    };

    const handleDelete = async (mode: PaymentMode) => {
        const result = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Delete Payment Mode?',
            text: `Are you sure you want to delete "${mode.name}"? Transactions using this payment mode will preserve the name string but lose the ID relation.`,
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonColor: '#ff6b6b',
            cancelButtonColor: '#555',
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes, delete it',
            background: '#1a1a2e',
            color: '#eee',
        });

        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            try {
                await axios.delete(`/api/payment-modes/${mode.id}`);
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: 'Deleted!',
                    text: 'Payment mode has been deleted.',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
                fetchModes();
            } catch (err: any) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Error',
                    text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to delete payment mode.',
                    background: '#1a1a2e',
                    color: '#eee',
                });
            }
        }
    };

    const filteredModes = modes.filter((m) =>
        m.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        m.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
    );

    const isAuthorized = user?.role === 'ADMIN' || user?.role === 'STAFF';

    return (
        <div className="container" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            {/* Header */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                <div>
                    <h1 style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', fontSize: '1.8rem', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        <CreditCard size={28} style={{ color: 'var(--primary)' }} />
                        Payment Modes
                    </h1>
                    <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.9rem', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                        Manage modes of payment available at your Point of Sale checkout.
                    </p>
                </div>
                {isAuthorized && (
                    <motion.button
                        className="btn btn-primary"
                        onClick={openCreateModal}
                        whileHover={{ scale: 1.02 }}
                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                        style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}
                    >
                        <Plus size={18} /> Add Payment Mode
                    </motion.button>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* Toolbar */}
            <div className="card" style={{ marginBottom: '20px', padding: '16px', display: 'flex', gap: '16px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <div className="search-box" style={{ flex: 1, maxWidth: '400px' }}>
                    <Search size={18} />
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Search payment modes..."
                        value={searchTerm}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                        style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', width: '100%', color: '#fff', outline: 'none' }}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* List */}
            {loading ? (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', padding: '40px' }}>
                    <div className="spinner" />
                </div>
            ) : filteredModes.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="card" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                    <CreditCard size={48} style={{ opacity: 0.3, marginBottom: '12px' }} />
                    <p>No payment modes found.</p>
                </div>
            ) : (
                <div className="table-responsive">
                    <table className="table">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>Name</th>
                                <th>Description</th>
                                <th>Status</th>
                                <th>Created At</th>
                                {isAuthorized && <th style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>Actions</th>}
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {filteredModes.map((mode) => (
                                <tr key={mode.id}>
                                    <td>
                                        <strong>{mode.name}</strong>
                                    </td>
                                    <td style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>{mode.description || '-'}</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <span className={`badge ${mode.isActive ? 'badge-success' : 'badge-danger'}`} style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                                            {mode.isActive ? <Check size={12} /> : <X size={12} />}
                                            {mode.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                                        {new Date(mode.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                                    </td>
                                    {isAuthorized && (
                                        <td style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                                                <button
                                                    className="btn btn-icon btn-ghost"
                                                    onClick={() => openEditModal(mode)}
                                                    title="Edit"
                                                >
                                                    <Edit3 size={16} />
                                                </button>
                                                <button
                                                    className="btn btn-icon btn-ghost text-danger"
                                                    onClick={() => handleDelete(mode)}
                                                    title="Delete"
                                                >
                                                    <Trash2 size={16} />
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                    )}
                                </tr>
                            ))}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal Overlay / Form Drawer */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {isOpen && (
                    <>
                        {/* Backdrop */}
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 0.6 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                            style={{
                                position: 'fixed',
                                top: 0,
                                left: 0,
                                width: '100vw',
                                height: '100vh',
                                background: '#000',
                                zIndex: 1000,
                            }}
                        />

                        {/* Modal Container */}
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, y: 20 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, y: 20 }}
                            transition={{ type: 'spring', damping: 25, stiffness: 350 }}
                            style={{
                                position: 'fixed',
                                top: '15%',
                                left: 'calc(50% - 225px)',
                                width: '450px',
                                background: '#1a1a2e',
                                border: '1px solid #333',
                                borderRadius: '12px',
                                padding: '24px',
                                zIndex: 1001,
                                boxShadow: '0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
                                color: '#eee',
                            }}
                        >
                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                                <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.2rem', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                                    {editingMode ? 'Edit Payment Mode' : 'Add Payment Mode'}
                                </h3>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                                    style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: '#aaa', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                                >
                                    <X size={20} />
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            {error && (
                                <div className="alert alert-danger" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', marginBottom: '16px', padding: '10px', background: 'rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.1)', border: '1px solid var(--red)', borderRadius: '6px', color: 'var(--red)', fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>
                                    <AlertCircle size={16} />
                                    <span>{error}</span>
                                </div>
                            )}

                            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
                                <div className="form-group" style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: '6px', fontSize: '0.9rem', fontWeight: 500 }}>Name *</label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={name}
                                        onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="e.g. Cash, GCash, Card"
                                        required
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px', background: '#16213e', border: '1px solid #333', borderRadius: '6px', color: '#fff' }}
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div className="form-group" style={{ marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                                    <label style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: '6px', fontSize: '0.9rem', fontWeight: 500 }}>Description</label>
                                    <textarea
                                        value={description}
                                        onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="Enter details..."
                                        rows={3}
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '10px', background: '#16213e', border: '1px solid #333', borderRadius: '6px', color: '#fff', fontFamily: 'inherit' }}
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div className="form-group" style={{ marginBottom: '24px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        id="active-toggle"
                                        checked={isActive}
                                        onChange={(e) => setIsActive(e.target.checked)}
                                        style={{ width: '16px', height: '16px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                                    />
                                    <label htmlFor="active-toggle" style={{ fontSize: '0.9rem', cursor: 'pointer', userSelect: 'none' }}>
                                        Active (Available at POS checkout)
                                    </label>
                                </div>

                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: '12px' }}>
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        className="btn btn-ghost"
                                        onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                                    >
                                        Cancel
                                    </button>
                                    <button
                                        type="submit"
                                        className="btn btn-primary"
                                    >
                                        {editingMode ? 'Update' : 'Create'}
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </motion.div>
                    </>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    );
}


================================================================================
FILE: client\src\pages\POS.tsx
================================================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import axios from 'axios';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useBranding } from '../context/BrandingContext';
import {
    Search,
    ShoppingCart,
    Plus,
    Minus,
    Trash2,
    CreditCard,
    Barcode,
    Camera,
    Printer,
    X,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Product {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    price: number;
    costPrice: number;
    retailPrice: number;
    taxRate: number;
    taxInclusive: boolean;
    barcode: string;
    imageUrl: string | null;
    stockQty: number;
    category: { name: string };
    stockItems?: Array<{ id: number; serialNumber: string; status: string }>;
}

interface CartItem extends Product {
    quantity: number;
    selectedSerialNumbers: string[];
}

const generateSeriesNumber = (txnId: number, dateStr: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    const yyyy = date.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const formattedId = String(txnId).padStart(5, '0');
    return `AP-${yyyy}${mm}${dd}-${formattedId}`;
};

export default function POS() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const { hasPosPin, posPinLength, maintenanceMode } = useBranding();
    const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);

    const requestAdminAuth = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
        if (user?.role === 'ADMIN') {
            return true;
        }

        const promptText = hasPosPin 
            ? `Please enter the ${posPinLength}-digit Admin POS PIN or Admin password to authorize this void.` 
            : 'Please enter the Admin password to authorize this void.';

        const { value: inputVal } = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Admin Authorization Required',
            text: promptText,
            input: 'password',
            inputPlaceholder: hasPosPin ? 'Admin PIN or Password' : 'Admin Password',
            inputAttributes: {
                autocapitalize: 'off',
                autocorrect: 'off'
            },
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Verify',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            inputValidator: (value) => {
                if (!value) {
                    return 'Verification code/password is required!';
                }
            }
        });

        if (!inputVal) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            const verifyRes = await axios.post('/api/settings/verify-pin', { value: inputVal });
            if (verifyRes.data && verifyRes.data.valid) {
                return true;
            } else {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Access Denied',
                    text: 'Invalid Admin PIN or Password.',
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                    confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
                });
                return false;
            }
        } catch (err: any) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Verification Failed',
                text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Verification error',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
            });
            return false;
        }
    };
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
    const [discount, setDiscount] = useState(0);
    const [showScanner, setShowScanner] = useState(false);
    const [paymentModes, setPaymentModes] = useState<any[]>([]);
    const searchRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const scannerRef = useRef<any>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        axios.get('/api/products').then((r) => setProducts(r.data));
        axios.get('/api/payment-modes?active=true').then((r) => setPaymentModes(r.data));
    }, []);

    const filteredProducts = products.filter(
        (p) =>
            p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
            p.barcode.includes(search) ||
            p.stockItems?.some(s => s.status === 'AVAILABLE' && s.serialNumber.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
    );

    const addToCart = (product: Product, specificSn?: string) => {
        const availableSns = product.stockItems?.filter(s => s.status === 'AVAILABLE') || [];

        setCart((prev) => {
            const existing = prev.find((item) => item.id === product.id);

            let snToAttach = specificSn;
            if (!snToAttach) {
                const currentSelected = existing ? existing.selectedSerialNumbers : [];
                const firstFree = availableSns.find(s => !currentSelected.includes(s.serialNumber));
                if (firstFree) {
                    snToAttach = firstFree.serialNumber;
                }
            }

            if (existing) {
                if (existing.quantity >= product.stockQty) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'warning',
                        title: 'Insufficient Stock',
                        text: `Only ${product.stockQty} available`,
                        background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                        color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                        confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
                    });
                    return prev;
                }
                const updatedSns = [...existing.selectedSerialNumbers];
                if (snToAttach && !updatedSns.includes(snToAttach)) {
                    updatedSns.push(snToAttach);
                }
                return prev.map((item) =>
                    item.id === product.id ? { ...item, quantity: item.quantity + 1, selectedSerialNumbers: updatedSns } : item
                );
            }

            const initialSns = snToAttach ? [snToAttach] : [];
            return [...prev, { ...product, quantity: 1, selectedSerialNumbers: initialSns }];
        });
    };

    const updateQuantity = (productId: number, delta: number) => {
        setCart((prev) => {
            return prev
                .map((item) => {
                    if (item.id === productId) {
                        const newQty = item.quantity + delta;
                        if (newQty <= 0) {
                            Swal.fire({
                                icon: 'info',
                                title: 'To Void Item',
                                text: 'Please use the "Void" button to remove items from the cart.',
                                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
                                timer: 2500,
                            });
                            return item;
                        }
                        if (newQty > item.stockQty) {
                             Swal.fire({
                                 icon: 'warning',
                                 title: 'Max stock reached',
                                 text: `Only ${item.stockQty} available`,
                                 background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                                 color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                                 timer: 1500,
                                 showConfirmButton: false,
                             });
                            return item;
                        }

                        let updatedSns = [...item.selectedSerialNumbers];
                        if (delta > 0) {
                            const availableSns = item.stockItems?.filter(s => s.status === 'AVAILABLE') || [];
                            const firstFree = availableSns.find(s => !updatedSns.includes(s.serialNumber));
                            if (firstFree) {
                                updatedSns.push(firstFree.serialNumber);
                            }
                        } else if (delta < 0) {
                            updatedSns.pop();
                        }

                        return { ...item, quantity: newQty, selectedSerialNumbers: updatedSns };
                    }
                    return item;
                })
                .filter(Boolean) as CartItem[];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = async (productId: number) => {
        const authorized = await requestAdminAuth();
        if (!authorized) return;
        setCart((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== productId));
    };

    // Computations for Prices and Tax
    const getPOSUnitPrice = (p: Product) => {
        const retail = p.retailPrice > 0 ? p.retailPrice : p.price;
        if (p.taxRate > 0 && !p.taxInclusive) {
            return retail * (1 + p.taxRate / 100);
        }
        return retail;
    };

    const netSubtotal = cart.reduce((sum, item) => {
        const retail = item.retailPrice > 0 ? item.retailPrice : item.price;
        return sum + retail * item.quantity;
    }, 0);

    const taxAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => {
        const retail = item.retailPrice > 0 ? item.retailPrice : item.price;
        const taxRate = item.taxRate || 0;
        if (taxRate > 0) {
            if (item.taxInclusive) {
                const netBase = retail / (1 + taxRate / 100);
                return sum + (retail - netBase) * item.quantity;
            } else {
                return sum + (retail * (taxRate / 100)) * item.quantity;
            }
        }
        return sum;
    }, 0);

    const exclusiveTaxAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => {
        const retail = item.retailPrice > 0 ? item.retailPrice : item.price;
        const taxRate = item.taxRate || 0;
        if (taxRate > 0 && !item.taxInclusive) {
            return sum + (retail * (taxRate / 100)) * item.quantity;
        }
        return sum;
    }, 0);

    const subtotal = netSubtotal + exclusiveTaxAmount;
    const total = subtotal - discount;

    const handleCheckout = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'info',
                title: 'Empty Cart',
                text: 'Add items before checkout',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            });
            return;
        }

        const result = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Confirm Payment',
            html: `
        <div style="text-align:left; font-family: var(--font); color: var(--text-primary);">
          <p style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Items: <strong>${cart.length}</strong></p>
          <p style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Subtotal: <strong>₱${netSubtotal.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
          ${exclusiveTaxAmount > 0 ? `<p style="margin-bottom: 8px;">VAT (Exclusive): <strong>₱${exclusiveTaxAmount.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>` : ''}
          ${taxAmount - exclusiveTaxAmount > 0 ? `<p style="margin-bottom: 8px; color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.9em;">VAT Included: ₱${(taxAmount - exclusiveTaxAmount).toFixed(2)}</p>` : ''}
          <p style="margin-bottom: 8px;">Discount: <strong>₱${discount.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
          <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 12px 0;" />
          <div style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 6px; font-size: 0.9rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-primary);">Payment Mode *</label>
            <select id="swal-payment-mode" style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); font-family: inherit; outline: none; cursor: pointer;">
              ${paymentModes.length === 0 
                ? '<option value="" style="background: var(--bg-secondary); color: var(--text-primary);">(No Payment Modes Configured)</option>' 
                : paymentModes.map(pm => `<option value="${pm.id}" style="background: var(--bg-secondary); color: var(--text-primary);">${pm.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 6px; font-size: 0.9rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-primary);">Amount Tendered *</label>
            <input type="number" id="swal-amount-tendered" step="0.01" min="${total.toFixed(2)}" value="${total.toFixed(2)}" oninput="
                const t = ${total};
                const val = parseFloat(this.value) || 0;
                const change = val >= t ? val - t : 0;
                document.getElementById('swal-change-amount').innerText = '₱' + change.toFixed(2);
            " style="width: 100%; padding: 10px; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); font-family: inherit; outline: none; box-sizing: border-box;" />
          </div>
          <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 12px 0;" />
          <p style="font-size: 1.3em; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 4px;"><span>Total:</span> <strong style="color: var(--green);">₱${total.toFixed(2)}</strong></p>
          <p style="font-size: 1.1em; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0;"><span>Change:</span> <strong style="color: var(--accent);" id="swal-change-amount">₱0.00</strong></p>
        </div>
      `,
            icon: 'question',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Process Payment',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            preConfirm: () => {
                const selectEl = document.getElementById('swal-payment-mode') as HTMLSelectElement;
                const paymentModeId = selectEl ? selectEl.value : '';
                if (!paymentModeId && paymentModes.length > 0) {
                    Swal.showValidationMessage('Please select a payment mode.');
                    return false;
                }
                const amountEl = document.getElementById('swal-amount-tendered') as HTMLInputElement;
                const amountTendered = amountEl ? parseFloat(amountEl.value) : 0;
                
                if (isNaN(amountTendered) || amountTendered < total - 0.001) {
                    Swal.showValidationMessage(`Amount tendered must be at least ₱${total.toFixed(2)}.`);
                    return false;
                }
                const changeAmount = amountTendered - total;
                return { paymentModeId, amountTendered, changeAmount };
            }
        });

        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            try {
                const res = await axios.post('/api/transactions', {
                    items: cart.map((item) => ({
                        productId: item.id,
                        quantity: item.quantity,
                        serialNumbers: item.selectedSerialNumbers,
                    })),
                    discount,
                    paymentModeId: result.value.paymentModeId ? parseInt(result.value.paymentModeId) : null,
                    amountTendered: result.value.amountTendered,
                    changeAmount: result.value.changeAmount,
                });

                // Show receipt
                const txn = res.data;
                await Swal.fire({
                    title: '✅ Payment Successful!',
                    html: `
            <div style="font-family: var(--font); color: var(--text-primary); text-align: left;">
              <!-- Tab Buttons -->
              <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 15px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); padding-bottom: 8px;">
                <button id="btn-tab-customer" onclick="
