FILE: client\src\pages\SalesHistory.tsx
================================================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Search, Receipt, Calendar, Eye, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useBranding } from '../context/BrandingContext';

interface Transaction {
    id: number;
    cashierId: number;
    totalAmount: number;
    taxAmount: number;
    discount: number;
    paymentModeId?: number | null;
    paymentModeName?: string | null;
    amountTendered?: number | null;
    changeAmount?: number | null;
    status: string;
    createdAt: string;
    cashier: { name: string; email: string; role: string };
    items: Array<{
        id: number;
        quantity: number;
        price: number;
        subtotal: number;
        product: { name: string; barcode: string; costPrice?: number };
        stockItems?: Array<{ id: number; serialNumber: string }>;
    }>;
}

const generateSeriesNumber = (txnId: number, dateStr: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    const yyyy = date.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const formattedId = String(txnId).padStart(5, '0');
    return `AP-${yyyy}${mm}${dd}-${formattedId}`;
};

export default function SalesHistory() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const { hasPosPin, posPinLength } = useBranding();
    const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<Transaction[]>([]);
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    const fetchTransactions = () => {
        setLoading(true);
        axios
            .get('/api/transactions')
            .then((r) => setTransactions(r.data))
            .catch(console.error)
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchTransactions();
    }, []);

    const filtered = transactions.filter(
        (t) =>
            t.id.toString().includes(search) ||
            generateSeriesNumber(t.id, t.createdAt).toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
            t.cashier.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
            t.items.some((i) =>
                i.product.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
                i.product.barcode.includes(search)
            )
    );

    const requestAdminAuth = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
        if (user?.role === 'ADMIN') {
            return true;
        }

        const promptText = hasPosPin 
            ? `Please enter the ${posPinLength}-digit Admin POS PIN or Admin password to authorize this void.` 
            : 'Please enter the Admin password to authorize this void.';

        const { value: inputVal } = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Admin Authorization Required',
            text: promptText,
            input: 'password',
            inputPlaceholder: hasPosPin ? 'Admin PIN or Password' : 'Admin Password',
            inputAttributes: {
                autocapitalize: 'off',
                autocorrect: 'off'
            },
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Verify',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            inputValidator: (value) => {
                if (!value) {
                    return 'Verification code/password is required!';
                }
            }
        });

        if (!inputVal) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            const verifyRes = await axios.post('/api/settings/verify-pin', { value: inputVal });
            if (verifyRes.data && verifyRes.data.valid) {
                return true;
            } else {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Access Denied',
                    text: 'Invalid Admin PIN or Password.',
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                    confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
                });
                return false;
            }
        } catch (err: any) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Verification Failed',
                text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Verification error',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
            });
            return false;
        }
    };

    const handleVoidTransaction = async (txnId: number) => {
        const authorized = await requestAdminAuth();
        if (!authorized) return;

        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Void Transaction?',
            text: 'Are you sure you want to void this transaction? This will restore the product stock quantities and release their serial numbers.',
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Void Transaction',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--red)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)'
        }).then(async (result) => {
            if (result.isConfirmed) {
                try {
                    await axios.post(`/api/transactions/${txnId}/void`);
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'success',
                        title: 'Voided!',
                        text: 'Transaction has been voided successfully.',
                        background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                        color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                        confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
                    });
                    fetchTransactions();
                } catch (err: any) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        icon: 'error',
                        title: 'Void Failed',
                        text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Something went wrong',
                        background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                        color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                        confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)'
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    };

    const viewReceipt = (txn: Transaction) => {
        Swal.fire({
            title: `Receipt Details`,
            html: `
        <div style="font-family: var(--font); color: var(--text-primary); text-align: left;">
          <!-- Tab Buttons -->
          <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 15px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); padding-bottom: 8px;">
            <button id="btn-tab-customer" onclick="
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-tab').forEach(t => t.style.display = 'none');
              document.getElementById('receipt-customer').style.display = 'block';
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-btn').forEach(b => { b.style.background = 'var(--bg-hover)'; b.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)'; });
              this.style.background = 'var(--accent)';
              this.style.color = '#fff';
            " class="receipt-btn" style="flex: 1; padding: 6px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;">Customer</button>
            
            <button id="btn-tab-merchant" onclick="
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-tab').forEach(t => t.style.display = 'none');
              document.getElementById('receipt-merchant').style.display = 'block';
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-btn').forEach(b => { b.style.background = 'var(--bg-hover)'; b.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)'; });
              this.style.background = 'var(--accent)';
              this.style.color = '#fff';
            " class="receipt-btn" style="flex: 1; padding: 6px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: var(--bg-hover); color: var(--text-primary); border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;">Cashier/Staff</button>
            
            ${user?.role === 'ADMIN' ? `
            <button id="btn-tab-audit" onclick="
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-tab').forEach(t => t.style.display = 'none');
              document.getElementById('receipt-audit').style.display = 'block';
              document.querySelectorAll('.receipt-btn').forEach(b => { b.style.background = 'var(--bg-hover)'; b.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)'; });
              this.style.background = 'var(--accent)';
              this.style.color = '#fff';
            " class="receipt-btn" style="flex: 1; padding: 6px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: var(--bg-hover); color: var(--text-primary); border: none; border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;">Admin Audit</button>
            ` : ''}
          </div>

          <!-- Customer Tab Content -->
          <div id="receipt-customer" class="receipt-tab" style="display: block; font-size: 0.9em;">
            <h4 style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 4px 0; font-size: 1.1em;">AZTECH POS</h4>
            <p style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 10px 0; font-size: 0.8em; color: var(--text-muted);">${txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 'VOIDED TRANSACTION' : 'Customer Copy'}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Receipt #:</strong> ${generateSeriesNumber(txn.id, txn.createdAt)}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Cashier:</strong> ${txn.cashier?.name || 'Unknown'}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Payment Mode:</strong> ${txn.paymentModeName || 'None'}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Date:</strong> ${new Date(txn.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Status:</strong> <span style="font-weight: bold; color: ${txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 'red' : 'var(--green)'};">${txn.status}</span></p>
            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 10px 0;" />
            ${txn.items.map((i) => `
              <div style="margin: 6px 0;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                  <span><b>${i.product?.name}</b> x${i.quantity}</span>
                  <span>₱${i.subtotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
                </div>
                ${i.stockItems && i.stockItems.length > 0 ? `
                  <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted); font-family: monospace; margin-top: 2px;">
                    S/Ns: ${i.stockItems.map((s) => s.serialNumber).join(', ')}
                  </div>
                ` : ''}
              </div>
            `).join('')}
            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 10px 0;" />
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Subtotal</span>
              <span>₱${(txn.totalAmount + txn.discount - txn.taxAmount).toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            ${txn.taxAmount > 0 ? `
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0; color: var(--text-muted);">
                <span>VAT</span>
                <span>₱${txn.taxAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
            ` : ''}
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Discount</span>
              <span>-₱${txn.discount.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 1.15em; font-weight: bold; margin-top: 8px;">
              <span>Total Paid</span>
              <span style="color: var(--green);">₱${txn.totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            ${txn.amountTendered !== null && txn.amountTendered !== undefined ? `
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Amount Tendered</span>
              <span>₱${txn.amountTendered.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Change</span>
              <span>₱${(txn.changeAmount || 0).toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            ` : ''}
          </div>

          <!-- Cashier/Staff Tab Content -->
          <div id="receipt-merchant" class="receipt-tab" style="display: none; font-size: 0.9em;">
            <h4 style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 4px 0; font-size: 1.1em;">AZTECH POS</h4>
            <p style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 10px 0; font-size: 0.8em; color: var(--text-muted);">${txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 'VOIDED TRANSACTION' : 'Merchant/Staff Copy'}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Receipt #:</strong> ${generateSeriesNumber(txn.id, txn.createdAt)}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Staff/Role:</strong> ${txn.cashier?.name || 'Unknown'} (${txn.cashier?.role || 'STAFF'})</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Payment Mode:</strong> ${txn.paymentModeName || 'None'}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Date:</strong> ${new Date(txn.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 10px 0;" />
            ${txn.items.map((i) => `
              <div style="margin: 6px 0;">
                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                  <span><b>${i.product?.name}</b> x${i.quantity}</span>
                  <span>₱${i.subtotal.toFixed(2)}</span>
                </div>
                ${i.stockItems && i.stockItems.length > 0 ? `
                  <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted); font-family: monospace; margin-top: 2px;">
                    S/Ns: ${i.stockItems.map((s) => s.serialNumber).join(', ')}
                  </div>
                ` : ''}
              </div>
            `).join('')}
            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 10px 0;" />
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0; font-weight: bold;">
              <span>Total Charged</span>
              <span>₱${txn.totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            ${txn.amountTendered !== null && txn.amountTendered !== undefined ? `
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Amount Tendered</span>
              <span>₱${txn.amountTendered.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 4px 0;">
              <span>Change</span>
              <span>₱${(txn.changeAmount || 0).toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            ` : ''}
            
            <div style="margin-top: 30px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px;">
              <div style="text-align: center;">
                <div style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--text-muted); width: 80%; margin: 0 auto 4px auto; height: 20px;"></div>
                <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Customer Signature</span>
              </div>
              <div style="text-align: center;">
                <div style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--text-muted); width: 80%; margin: 0 auto 4px auto; height: 20px;"></div>
                <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted);">Authorized Cashier: ${txn.cashier?.name || 'Unknown'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Admin Audit Tab Content -->
          <div id="receipt-audit" class="receipt-tab" style="display: none; font-size: 0.9em;">
            <h4 style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 4px 0; font-size: 1.1em;">AZTECH POS</h4>
            <p style="text-align: center; margin: 0 0 10px 0; font-size: 0.8em; color: var(--text-muted);">Internal Administration Audit Copy</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Receipt #:</strong> ${generateSeriesNumber(txn.id, txn.createdAt)}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Cashier ID:</strong> #${txn.cashierId} (${txn.cashier?.name || 'Unknown'})</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Payment Mode:</strong> ${txn.paymentModeName || 'None'} (ID: ${txn.paymentModeId || 'N/A'})</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Transaction ID:</strong> #${txn.id}</p>
            <p style="margin: 4px 0;"><strong>Date:</strong> ${new Date(txn.createdAt).toISOString()}</p>
            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); margin: 10px 0;" />
            
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 0.8rem; margin-bottom: 10px;">
              <thead>
                <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); text-align: left;">
                  <th style="padding: 4px 0;">Product</th>
                  <th style="padding: 4px 0; text-align: right;">Cost</th>
                  <th style="padding: 4px 0; text-align: right;">Retail</th>
                  <th style="padding: 4px 0; text-align: right;">Qty</th>
                  <th style="padding: 4px 0; text-align: right;">Margin</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${txn.items.map((i) => {
                  const cost = i.product?.costPrice || 0;
                  const retail = i.price || 0;
                  const itemCostTotal = cost * i.quantity;
                  const itemProfit = i.subtotal - itemCostTotal;
                  return `
                    <tr style="border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border);">
                      <td style="padding: 6px 0;"><b>${i.product?.name}</b></td>
                      <td style="padding: 6px 0; text-align: right;">₱${cost.toFixed(2)}</td>
                      <td style="padding: 6px 0; text-align: right;">₱${retail.toFixed(2)}</td>
                      <td style="padding: 6px 0; text-align: right;">x${i.quantity}</td>
                      <td style="padding: 6px 0; text-align: right; color: ${itemProfit >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'red'};">₱${itemProfit.toFixed(2)}</td>
                    </tr>
                  `;
                }).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            <div style="border-top: 1px solid var(--border); padding-top: 6px;">
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 2px 0;">
                <span>Total Gross Retail</span>
                <span>₱${(txn.totalAmount + txn.discount).toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 2px 0;">
                <span>Total Cost of Goods</span>
                <span>₱${txn.items.reduce((sum: number, i: any) => sum + (i.product?.costPrice || 0) * i.quantity, 0).toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 2px 0;">
                <span>Total Discount Given</span>
                <span style="color: red;">-₱${txn.discount.toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 2px 0;">
                <span>Total VAT collected</span>
                <span>₱${txn.taxAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
              <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border); margin: 6px 0;" />
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.1em;">
                <span>Net Profit Margin</span>
                <span style="color: var(--green);">₱${(
                  txn.totalAmount - 
                  txn.items.reduce((sum: number, i: any) => sum + (i.product?.costPrice || 0) * i.quantity, 0)
                ).toFixed(2)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
            confirmButtonText: 'Close',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            width: 480,
        });
    };

    return (
        <div className="page">
            <div className="page-header">
                <div>
                    <h1>Sales History</h1>
                    <p>{transactions.length} transactions</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="filters-bar">
                <div className="search-box">
                    <Search size={18} />
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Search by series #, cashier, product..."
                        value={search}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
                <div className="loading-container"><div className="spinner" /></div>
            ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="empty-state">
                    <Receipt size={48} />
                    <h3>No transactions found</h3>
                </div>
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    className="card"
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                >
                    <div className="table-container">
                        <table>
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th>Receipt Series</th>
                                    <th>Cashier</th>
                                    <th>Items</th>
                                    <th>Discount</th>
                                    <th>Total</th>
                                    <th>Payment Mode</th>
                                    <th>Date</th>
                                    <th>Status</th>
                                    <th>Actions</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {filtered.map((txn) => (
                                    <tr key={txn.id} style={{ opacity: txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 0.6 : 1 }}>
                                        <td><strong>{generateSeriesNumber(txn.id, txn.createdAt)}</strong></td>
                                        <td>
                                            {txn.cashier.name}
                                            <span style={{ fontSize: '0.65rem', marginLeft: '6px', padding: '2px 4px', background: 'var(--bg-hover)', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', borderRadius: '3px', textTransform: 'uppercase', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                                {txn.cashier.role}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>{txn.items.length} items</td>
                                        <td>{txn.discount > 0 ? <span className="text-red">-₱{txn.discount.toFixed(2)}</span> : '-'}</td>
                                        <td>
                                            <span className={txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 'text-red' : 'text-green'} style={{ textDecoration: txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? 'line-through' : 'none' }}>
                                                ₱{txn.totalAmount.toFixed(2)}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            <span style={{ fontSize: '0.85rem', padding: '4px 8px', background: 'rgba(108, 99, 255, 0.15)', color: '#a29bfe', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid rgba(108, 99, 255, 0.3)' }}>
                                                {txn.paymentModeName || 'None'}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            <div className="date-cell">
                                                <Calendar size={14} />
                                                {new Date(txn.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            {txn.status === 'VOIDED' ? (
                                                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', padding: '2px 6px', background: 'rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.15)', color: '#ff6b6b', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid rgba(255, 107, 107, 0.3)', textTransform: 'uppercase', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                                    Voided
                                                </span>
                                            ) : (
                                                <span style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', padding: '2px 6px', background: 'rgba(107, 203, 119, 0.15)', color: '#2b8a3e', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid rgba(107, 203, 119, 0.3)', textTransform: 'uppercase', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                                                    Completed
                                                </span>
                                            )}
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                                                <button className="btn-icon btn-view" onClick={() => viewReceipt(txn)} title="View Receipt Copies">
                                                    <Eye size={16} />
                                                </button>
                                                {(user?.role === 'ADMIN' || user?.role === 'STAFF') && txn.status !== 'VOIDED' && (
                                                    <button className="btn-icon btn-delete" onClick={() => handleVoidTransaction(txn.id)} title="Void Transaction">
                                                        <Trash2 size={16} />
                                                    </button>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


================================================================================
FILE: client\src\pages\Settings.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { useBranding } from '../context/BrandingContext';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { Save, Upload, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Settings() {
    const { name, subtitle, logoIcon, logoUrl, posPinLength, hasPosPin, emailDomain, maintenanceMode, updateSettings } = useBranding();
    const [appName, setAppName] = useState(name);
    const [appSubtitle, setAppSubtitle] = useState(subtitle);
    const [appLogoIcon, setAppLogoIcon] = useState(logoIcon);
    const [maintenanceModeState, setMaintenanceModeState] = useState<boolean>(maintenanceMode || false);
    const [logoFile, setLogoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const [logoPreview, setLogoPreview] = useState<string | null>(logoUrl);
    const [removeLogo, setRemoveLogo] = useState(false);
    const [isDragActive, setIsDragActive] = useState(false);
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    // POS PIN states
    const [pinLength, setPinLength] = useState<number>(posPinLength || 4);
    const [pinValue, setPinValue] = useState<string>('');
    const [shouldClearPin, setShouldClearPin] = useState<boolean>(false);

    // Default Email Domain state
    const [domainName, setDomainName] = useState<string>(emailDomain || 'shadow.com');

    // Sync state when context values load
    useEffect(() => {
        setAppName(name);
        setAppSubtitle(subtitle);
        setAppLogoIcon(logoIcon);
        setLogoPreview(logoUrl);
        setPinLength(posPinLength || 4);
        setDomainName(emailDomain || 'shadow.com');
        setMaintenanceModeState(maintenanceMode || false);
    }, [name, subtitle, logoIcon, logoUrl, posPinLength, emailDomain, maintenanceMode]);

    // Cleanup object URLs to prevent memory leaks
    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            if (logoPreview && logoPreview.startsWith('blob:')) {
                URL.revokeObjectURL(logoPreview);
            }
        };
    }, [logoPreview]);

    const handleFileSelect = (file: File) => {
        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Invalid File',
                text: 'Please upload an image file.',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            });
            return;
        }
        setLogoFile(file);
        setLogoPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
        setRemoveLogo(false);
    };

    const handleRemoveLogo = () => {
        setLogoFile(null);
        setLogoPreview(null);
        setRemoveLogo(true);
        if (fileInputRef.current) {
            fileInputRef.current.value = '';
        }
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSaving(true);

        // Validate PIN length if a new PIN value is provided
        if (pinValue && pinValue.length !== pinLength) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Invalid PIN Length',
                text: `Please enter a valid ${pinLength}-digit PIN.`,
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            });
            setSaving(false);
            return;
        }

        try {
            // Determine PIN payload
            let targetPin: string | null | undefined = undefined;
            if (shouldClearPin) {
                targetPin = ''; // Clearing PIN
            } else if (pinValue) {
                targetPin = pinValue;
            }

            // Sanitize email domain
            let cleanDomain = domainName.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (cleanDomain.startsWith('@')) {
                cleanDomain = cleanDomain.substring(1);
            }
            if (!cleanDomain) {
                cleanDomain = 'shadow.com';
            }

            await updateSettings(appName, appSubtitle, appLogoIcon, logoFile, removeLogo, targetPin, pinLength, cleanDomain, maintenanceModeState);
            
            await Swal.fire({
                icon: 'success',
                title: 'Settings Saved!',
                text: 'System settings have been updated successfully.',
                timer: 1500,
                showConfirmButton: false,
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            });

            setLogoFile(null);
            setPinValue('');
            setShouldClearPin(false);
        } catch (err: any) {
            Swal.fire({
                icon: 'error',
                title: 'Error',
                text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Something went wrong',
                background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            });
        } finally {
            setSaving(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="page">
            <div className="page-header">
                <div>
                    <h1>System Settings</h1>
                    <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>Configure global application branding and security</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <motion.div
                className="card form-card"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                style={{ maxWidth: '600px', margin: '0 auto' }}
            >
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Application Name *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={appName}
                            onChange={(e) => setAppName(e.target.value)}
                            required
                            placeholder="e.g. Shadow Apollo"
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Subtitle / Description *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={appSubtitle}
                            onChange={(e) => setAppSubtitle(e.target.value)}
                            required
                            placeholder="e.g. Inventory & POS System"
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Custom Brand Logo Image</label>
                        <div
                            onDragOver={(e) => {
                                e.preventDefault();
                                setIsDragActive(true);
                            }}
                            onDragLeave={() => setIsDragActive(false)}
                            onDrop={(e) => {
                                e.preventDefault();
                                setIsDragActive(false);
                                if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
                                    handleFileSelect(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
                                }
                            }}
                            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                            style={{
                                border: isDragActive ? '2px dashed var(--accent)' : '2px dashed var(--border)',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                padding: '30px',
                                textAlign: 'center',
                                background: isDragActive ? 'var(--bg-hover)' : 'var(--bg-primary)',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
                                display: 'flex',
                                flexDirection: 'column',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                gap: '10px'
                            }}
                        >
                            <input
                                type="file"
                                ref={fileInputRef}
                                onChange={(e) => {
                                    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
                                        handleFileSelect(e.target.files[0]);
                                    }
                                }}
                                accept="image/*"
                                style={{ display: 'none' }}
                            />
                            
                            {logoPreview ? (
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                                    <img
                                        src={logoPreview}
                                        alt="Logo Preview"
                                        style={{ width: '80px', height: '80px', borderRadius: '12px', objectFit: 'cover', border: '1px solid var(--border)' }}
                                    />
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        className="btn btn-ghost"
                                        onClick={handleRemoveLogo}
                                        style={{ color: '#ff4d4d', padding: '4px 8px', fontSize: '0.9em' }}
                                    >
                                        <Trash2 size={16} /> Remove Custom Logo
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ) : (
                                <>
                                    <Upload size={32} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
                                    <div>
                                        <span style={{ color: 'var(--accent)', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>Click to upload</span> or drag and drop
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.8em' }}>PNG, JPG, SVG, or WEBP (max. 5MB)</div>
                                </>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Fallback Logo Icon (Emoji or single character) *</label>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                maxLength={2}
                                value={appLogoIcon}
                                onChange={(e) => setAppLogoIcon(e.target.value)}
                                required
                                placeholder="⚡"
                                style={{ width: '80px', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '1.5em' }}
                            />
                            <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.9em' }}>
                                Used if no custom brand image logo is uploaded above.
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div style={{ marginTop: '10px', display: 'flex', gap: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                            {['⚡', '🏪', '📦', '🛍️', '💻', '💼', '🚀', '🛒', '💰'].map((emoji) => (
                                <button
                                    key={emoji}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setAppLogoIcon(emoji)}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '8px 12px',
                                        background: appLogoIcon === emoji ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--bg-primary)',
                                        color: appLogoIcon === emoji ? '#fff' : 'var(--text-primary)',
                                        border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        fontSize: '1.2em',
                                        transition: 'background 0.2s',
                                    }}
                                >
                                    {emoji}
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <hr style={{ border: '0', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', margin: '10px 0' }} />
                    
                    <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.1rem', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>Default Email Suffix</h3>
                    
                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Default Email Domain for Cashiers/Users</label>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                            <span style={{ padding: '10px 12px', background: 'var(--bg-hover)', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRight: 'none', borderRadius: '8px 0 0 8px', fontSize: '1rem' }}>@</span>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={domainName}
                                onChange={(e) => setDomainName(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="e.g. shadow.com"
                                style={{ borderRadius: '0 8px 8px 0' }}
                            />
                        </div>
                        <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.8rem', marginTop: '6px' }}>
                            New users created without an email suffix will automatically be assigned this domain.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <hr style={{ border: '0', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', margin: '10px 0' }} />
                    
                    <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.1rem', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>POS Security PIN Setup</h3>
                    
                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>PIN Code Digits</label>
                        <select 
                            value={pinLength} 
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                const len = Number(e.target.value);
                                setPinLength(len);
                                setPinValue('');
                            }}
                        >
                            <option value="4">4-Digit PIN</option>
                            <option value="6">6-Digit PIN</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>

                    <div className="form-group">
                        <label>Admin POS PIN Code</label>
                        <input
                            type="password"
                            maxLength={pinLength}
                            value={pinValue}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                const val = e.target.value;
                                if (val === '' || /^\d+$/.test(val)) {
                                    setPinValue(val);
                                }
                            }}
                            placeholder={hasPosPin ? "•••••••• (PIN Configured)" : `Enter ${pinLength}-digit PIN`}
                        />
                        <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '0.8rem', marginTop: '6px' }}>
                            {hasPosPin 
                                ? "A POS security PIN is currently active. To update it, type a new PIN. To keep it, leave this field blank."
                                : "If set, cashiers will be prompted for this PIN (or the Admin's login password) when performing Void actions."
                            }
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    {hasPosPin && (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <input 
                                type="checkbox" 
                                id="clear-pin-checkbox" 
                                checked={shouldClearPin} 
                                onChange={(e) => {
                                    setShouldClearPin(e.target.checked);
                                    if (e.target.checked) setPinValue('');
                                }}
                            />
                            <label htmlFor="clear-pin-checkbox" style={{ fontSize: '0.85rem', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                                Remove POS PIN security verification (Allow any cashier to void without auth)
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <hr style={{ border: '0', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)', margin: '10px 0' }} />
                    
                    <h3 style={{ fontSize: '1.1rem', fontWeight: '600', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>System Maintenance Mode</h3>
                    
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', background: 'var(--bg-primary)', padding: '16px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
                        <input 
                            type="checkbox" 
                            id="maintenance-mode-checkbox" 
                            checked={maintenanceModeState} 
                            onChange={(e) => setMaintenanceModeState(e.target.checked)}
                            style={{ width: '20px', height: '20px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        />
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
                            <label htmlFor="maintenance-mode-checkbox" style={{ fontSize: '0.95rem', fontWeight: '600', cursor: 'pointer', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>
                                Enable Inventory Check / Price Check Only Mode
                            </label>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '0.8rem', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
                                When enabled, cashiers can search products and check prices, but payment processing (checkout) is disabled.
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="form-actions" style={{ marginTop: '10px' }}>
                        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" disabled={saving}>
                            <Save size={18} /> {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Settings'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </motion.div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: client\src\pages\UserManagement.tsx

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import axios from 'axios';
import Swal from 'sweetalert2';
import { Plus, Edit3, Trash2, Users, Shield, UserCheck, UserCog } from 'lucide-react';
import { useBranding } from '../context/BrandingContext';

interface User {
    id: number;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    role: string;
    createdAt: string;
}

export default function UserManagement() {
    const { emailDomain } = useBranding();
    const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    const fetchUsers = async () => {
        try {
            const res = await axios.get('/api/users');
            setUsers(res.data);
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(err);
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        fetchUsers();
    }, []);

    const openUserModal = async (user?: User) => {
        const EYE_SVG = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M2.062 12.348a1 1 0 0 1 0-.696 10.75 10.75 0 0 1 19.876 0 1 1 0 0 1 0 .696 10.75 10.75 0 0 1-19.876 0z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg>`;
        const EYE_OFF_SVG = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M9.88 9.88a3 3 0 1 0 4.24 4.24"/><path d="M10.73 5.08A10.43 10.43 0 0 1 12 5c7 0 10 7 10 7a13.16 13.16 0 0 1-1.67 2.68"/><path d="M6.61 6.61A13.52 13.52 0 0 0 2 12s3 7 10 7a9.74 9.74 0 0 0 5.39-1.61"/><line x1="2" y1="2" x2="22" y2="22"/></svg>`;

        const { value: formValues } = await Swal.fire({
            title: user ? 'Edit User' : 'Create User',
            html: `
        <div style="text-align:left; font-family: var(--font); color: var(--text-primary);">
          <div style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <label style="display:block; margin-bottom:4px; font-size:0.85em; color:var(--text-secondary);">Name</label>
            <input id="swal-name" class="swal2-input" placeholder="Full name" value="${user?.name || ''}" style="margin:0; width:100%; box-sizing:border-box; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border-color: var(--border);">
          </div>
          <div style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <label style="display:block; margin-bottom:4px; font-size:0.85em; color:var(--text-secondary);">Email</label>
            <input id="swal-email" class="swal2-input" type="email" placeholder="username@${emailDomain}" value="${user?.email || ''}" style="margin:0; width:100%; box-sizing:border-box; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border-color: var(--border);">
            <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 4px;">If no domain is specified, @${emailDomain} will be appended automatically.</div>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-bottom: 12px;">
            <label style="display:block; margin-bottom:4px; font-size:0.85em; color:var(--text-secondary);">${user ? 'New Password (leave blank to keep)' : 'Password'}</label>
            <div style="position: relative; display: flex; align-items: center;">
              <input id="swal-password" class="swal2-input" type="password" placeholder="••••••••" style="margin:0; width:100%; box-sizing:border-box; padding-right: 42px; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border-color: var(--border);">
              <button id="swal-toggle-password" type="button" style="position: absolute; right: 14px; background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer; color: var(--text-secondary); display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0; outline: none; transition: color 0.2s ease;">
                ${EYE_SVG}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label style="display:block; margin-bottom:4px; font-size:0.85em; color:var(--text-secondary);">Role</label>
            <select id="swal-role" class="swal2-select" style="margin:0; width:100%; box-sizing:border-box; padding: 8px; background: var(--bg-primary); color: var(--text-primary); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 8px;">
              <option value="CASHIER" ${user?.role === 'CASHIER' ? 'selected' : ''}>Cashier</option>
              <option value="STAFF" ${user?.role === 'STAFF' ? 'selected' : ''}>Staff</option>
              <option value="ADMIN" ${user?.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'selected' : ''}>Admin</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
            focusConfirm: false,
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonText: user ? 'Update' : 'Create',
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            preConfirm: () => {
                const name = (document.getElementById('swal-name') as HTMLInputElement).value.trim();
                let email = (document.getElementById('swal-email') as HTMLInputElement).value.trim();
                const password = (document.getElementById('swal-password') as HTMLInputElement).value;
                const role = (document.getElementById('swal-role') as HTMLSelectElement).value;
                
                if (!name || !email) {
                    Swal.showValidationMessage('Name and email are required');
                    return;
                }
                
                // Append email domain automatically if not present
                if (!email.includes('@')) {
                    email = `${email}@${emailDomain}`;
                }
                
                if (!user && !password) {
                    Swal.showValidationMessage('Password is required for new users');
                    return;
                }
                
                return { name, email, password, role };
            },
            didOpen: () => {
                const passwordInput = document.getElementById('swal-password') as HTMLInputElement;
                const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('swal-toggle-password') as HTMLButtonElement;
                if (passwordInput && toggleBtn) {
                    toggleBtn.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
                        toggleBtn.style.color = 'var(--text-primary)';
                    });
                    toggleBtn.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                        toggleBtn.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
                    });
                    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';
                        passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';
                        toggleBtn.innerHTML = isPassword ? EYE_OFF_SVG : EYE_SVG;
                    });
                }
            },
        });

        if (formValues) {
            try {
                const data: any = { name: formValues.name, email: formValues.email, role: formValues.role };
                if (formValues.password) data.password = formValues.password;

                if (user) {
                    await axios.put(`/api/users/${user.id}`, data);
                } else {
                    await axios.post('/api/users', data);
                }

                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: user ? 'Updated!' : 'Created!',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                });
                fetchUsers();
            } catch (err: any) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Error',
                    text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Something went wrong',
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                    confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
                });
            }
        }
    };

    const deleteUser = async (user: User) => {
        const result = await Swal.fire({
            title: 'Delete User?',
            text: `Are you sure you want to delete "${user.name}"?`,
            icon: 'warning',
            showCancelButton: true,
            confirmButtonColor: 'var(--red)',
            cancelButtonColor: 'var(--bg-hover)',
            background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
            color: 'var(--text-primary)',
            confirmButtonText: 'Yes, Delete'
        });

        if (result.isConfirmed) {
            try {
                await axios.delete(`/api/users/${user.id}`);
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'success',
                    title: 'Deleted!',
                    timer: 1500,
                    showConfirmButton: false,
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                });
                fetchUsers();
            } catch (err: any) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: 'Error',
                    text: err.response?.data?.error || 'Something went wrong',
                    background: 'var(--bg-secondary)',
                    color: 'var(--text-primary)',
                    confirmButtonColor: 'var(--accent)',
                });
            }
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="page">
            <div className="page-header">
                <div>
                    <h1>User Management</h1>
                    <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>Control system user access levels</p>
                </div>
                <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => openUserModal()}>
                    <Plus size={18} /> Add User
                </button>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
                <div className="loading-container"><div className="spinner" /></div>
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    className="card"
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                >
                    <div className="table-container">
                        <table>
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th>User</th>
                                    <th>Email</th>
                                    <th>Role</th>
                                    <th>Created At</th>
                                    <th>Actions</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {users.map((u) => (
                                    <tr key={u.id}>
                                        <td>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                                                <div style={{
                                                    width: '32px',
                                                    height: '32px',
                                                    borderRadius: '50%',
                                                    background: 'var(--bg-hover)',
                                                    display: 'flex',
                                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                                    justifyContent: 'center',
                                                    color: 'var(--accent)',
                                                    fontWeight: 'bold'
                                                }}>
                                                    {u.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                                                </div>
                                                <strong>{u.name}</strong>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>{u.email}</td>
                                        <td>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
                                                {u.role === 'ADMIN' ? (
                                                    <Shield size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
                                                ) : u.role === 'STAFF' ? (
                                                    <UserCog size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--green)' }} />
                                                ) : (
                                                    <UserCheck size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }} />
                                                )}
                                                <span style={{
                                                    fontSize: '0.8rem',
                                                    fontWeight: 'bold',
                                                    color: u.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'var(--accent)' : u.role === 'STAFF' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text-secondary)'
                                                }}>{u.role}</span>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>{new Date(u.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                                        <td>
                                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                                                <button className="btn-icon btn-edit" onClick={() => openUserModal(u)} title="Edit User">
                                                    <Edit3 size={16} />
                                                </button>
                                                <button className="btn-icon btn-delete" onClick={() => deleteUser(u)} title="Delete User">
                                                    <Trash2 size={16} />
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                ))}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: server\src\index.ts

import express from 'express';
import cors from 'cors';
import path from 'path';
import dotenv from 'dotenv';

// Load .env relative to this directory so it's always found regardless of process cwd
dotenv.config({ path: path.join(__dirname, '../.env') });

import authRoutes from './routes/auth';
import productRoutes from './routes/products';
import categoryRoutes from './routes/categories';
import transactionRoutes from './routes/transactions';
import userRoutes from './routes/users';
import settingsRoutes from './routes/settings';
import excelRoutes from './routes/excel';
import paymentModeRoutes from './routes/paymentModes';
import auditLogRoutes from './routes/auditLogs';
import inventoryAuditRoutes from './routes/inventoryAudits';

const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3001;

// Middleware
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));

// Static files for uploads
app.use('/uploads', express.static(path.join(__dirname, '../uploads')));

// Routes
app.use('/api/auth', authRoutes);
app.use('/api/products', productRoutes);
app.use('/api/categories', categoryRoutes);
app.use('/api/transactions', transactionRoutes);
app.use('/api/users', userRoutes);
app.use('/api/settings', settingsRoutes);
app.use('/api/excel', excelRoutes);
app.use('/api/payment-modes', paymentModeRoutes);
app.use('/api/audit-logs', auditLogRoutes);
app.use('/api/inventory-audits', inventoryAuditRoutes);

// Health check
app.get('/api/health', (_req, res) => {
    // Triggering nodemon restart to load new Prisma client
    res.json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() });
});

// Error handling middleware
app.use((err: any, _req: express.Request, res: express.Response, _next: express.NextFunction) => {
    console.error('Unhandled error:', err);
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
});

app.listen(Number(PORT), '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`🚀 Server running on http://localhost:${PORT}`);
});

export default app;



================================================================================
FILE: server\src\lib\barcode.ts
================================================================================

import bwipjs from 'bwip-js';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';

const BARCODE_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '../../uploads/barcodes');

export function generateBarcodeString(): string {
    const timestamp = Date.now().toString().slice(-8);
    const random = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000).toString().padStart(4, '0');
    return `${timestamp}${random}`;
}

export async function generateBarcodeImage(barcodeText: string): Promise<string> {
    if (!fs.existsSync(BARCODE_DIR)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(BARCODE_DIR, { recursive: true });
    }

    const filename = `barcode-${barcodeText}.png`;
    const filepath = path.join(BARCODE_DIR, filename);

    const png = await bwipjs.toBuffer({
        bcid: 'code128',
        text: barcodeText,
        scale: 3,
        height: 10,
        includetext: true,
        textxalign: 'center',
    });

    fs.writeFileSync(filepath, png);
    return `/uploads/barcodes/${filename}`;
}

================================================================================
FILE: server\src\middleware\auth.ts
================================================================================

import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';

export interface AuthRequest extends Request {
    user?: {
        id: number;
        email: string;
        role: string;
        name: string;
    };
}

export function authenticate(req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
    const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'shadow-apollo-secret';
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
    if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        res.status(401).json({ error: 'Access denied. No token provided.' });
        return;
    }

    const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1];
    try {
        const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as AuthRequest['user'];
        req.user = decoded;
        next();
    } catch {
        res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or expired token.' });
    }
}

export function authorize(...roles: string[]) {
    return (req: AuthRequest, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {
        if (!req.user || !roles.includes(req.user.role)) {
            res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden. Insufficient permissions.' });
            return;
        }
        next();
    };
}


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\middleware\upload.ts
================================================================================

import multer from 'multer';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';

const UPLOAD_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '../../uploads/products');

if (!fs.existsSync(UPLOAD_DIR)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(UPLOAD_DIR, { recursive: true });
}

const storage = multer.diskStorage({
    destination: (_req, _file, cb) => {
        cb(null, UPLOAD_DIR);
    },
    filename: (_req, file, cb) => {
        const uniqueSuffix = Date.now() + '-' + Math.round(Math.random() * 1e9);
        cb(null, uniqueSuffix + path.extname(file.originalname));
    },
});

const fileFilter = (_req: any, file: Express.Multer.File, cb: multer.FileFilterCallback) => {
    const allowedTypes = /jpeg|jpg|png|gif|webp/;
    const ext = allowedTypes.test(path.extname(file.originalname).toLowerCase());
    const mime = allowedTypes.test(file.mimetype);
    if (ext && mime) {
        cb(null, true);
    } else {
        cb(new Error('Only image files are allowed'));
    }
};

export const upload = multer({
    storage,
    fileFilter,
    limits: { fileSize: 5 * 1024 * 1024 }, // 5MB
});

import express from "express";
import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
import { authenticate } from "../middleware/auth";

const router = express.Router();
const prisma = new PrismaClient();

// Get all audit logs
router.get("/", authenticate, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const logs = await prisma.auditLog.findMany({
            orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
            include: {
                user: {
                    select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true }
                }
            },
            take: 500 // Limit to last 500 for performance
        });
        res.json(logs);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch audit logs:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch audit logs" });
    }
});

export default router;


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\routes\auth.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { logAudit } from '../utils/audit';


const router = Router();

router.post('/login', async (req, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { email, password } = req.body;
        const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET || 'shadow-apollo-secret';

        if (!email || !password) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required.' });
            return;
        }

        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
        if (!user) {
            res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password.' });
            return;
        }

        const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
        if (!isValid) {
            res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password.' });
            return;
        }

        const token = jwt.sign(
            { id: user.id, email: user.email, role: user.role, name: user.name },
            JWT_SECRET,
            { expiresIn: '24h' }
        );

        await logAudit(user.id, 'LOGIN', 'User', user.id, { email: user.email });


        res.json({
            token,
            user: {
                id: user.id,
                name: user.name,
                email: user.email,
                role: user.role,
            },
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Login error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error.' });
    }
});

export default router;
