import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// GET all categories
router.get('/', authenticate, async (_req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const categories = await prisma.category.findMany({
            include: { _count: { select: { products: true } } },
            orderBy: { name: 'asc' },
        });
        res.json(categories);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get categories error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch categories.' });
    }
});

// POST create category
router.post('/', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { name } = req.body;
        if (!name) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Category name is required.' });
            return;
        }
        const category = await prisma.category.create({ data: { name } });
        res.status(201).json(category);
    } catch (error: any) {
        if (error.code === 'P2002') {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Category already exists.' });
            return;
        }
        console.error('Create category error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to create category.' });
    }
});

// PUT update category
router.put('/:id', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { name } = req.body;
        const category = await prisma.category.update({
            where: { id: Number(req.params.id) },
            data: { name },
        });
        res.json(category);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Update category error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to update category.' });
    }
});

// DELETE category
router.delete('/:id', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        await prisma.category.delete({ where: { id: Number(req.params.id) } });
        res.json({ message: 'Category deleted successfully.' });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Delete category error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to delete category.' });
    }
});

export default router;


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\routes\excel.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import multer from 'multer';
import * as xlsx from 'xlsx';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// Configure memory storage for Multer file uploads
const storage = multer.memoryStorage();
const upload = multer({
    storage,
    limits: { fileSize: 10 * 1024 * 1024 }, // 10MB limit
});

// GET export Excel inventory (Protected - ADMIN & STAFF)
router.get('/export', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (_req, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const categories = await prisma.category.findMany({ orderBy: { name: 'asc' } });
        const products = (await prisma.product.findMany({
            include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
            orderBy: { name: 'asc' },
        })) as any[];

        // 1. Map Categories to rows
        const categoryRows = categories.map((c) => ({
            'Category Name': c.name,
        }));

        // 2. Map Products to rows
        const productRows = products.map((p) => ({
            'Barcode': p.barcode,
            'Product Name': p.name,
            'Description': p.description || '',
            'Price': p.price,
            'Cost Price': p.costPrice,
            'Retail Price': p.retailPrice,
            'Tax Rate': p.taxRate,
            'Tax Inclusive': p.taxInclusive ? 'Yes' : 'No',
            'Stock Quantity': p.stockQty,
            'Category Name': p.category.name,
            'Serial Numbers': ((p as any).stockItems || [])
                .filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE')
                .map((s: any) => s.serialNumber)
                .join(', '),
        }));

        // 3. Create a new Workbook
        const wb = xlsx.utils.book_new();

        // 4. Create Worksheets
        const wsCategories = xlsx.utils.json_to_sheet(categoryRows);
        const wsProducts = xlsx.utils.json_to_sheet(productRows);

        // 5. Append Worksheets to Workbook
        xlsx.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsCategories, 'Categories');
        xlsx.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsProducts, 'Products');

        // 6. Write Workbook to binary buffer
        const buffer = xlsx.write(wb, { type: 'buffer', bookType: 'xlsx' });

        // 7. Send workbook buffer as file attachment response
        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet');
        res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename=inventory.xlsx');
        res.send(buffer);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to export inventory:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to export inventory' });
    }
});

// POST import Excel inventory (Protected - ADMIN & STAFF)
router.post('/import', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), upload.single('file'), async (req: any, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        if (!req.file) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Please upload an Excel file.' });
            return;
        }

        // Parse workbook from buffer
        const workbook = xlsx.read(req.file.buffer, { type: 'buffer' });

        let categoriesUpserted = 0;
        let productsCreated = 0;
        let productsUpdated = 0;

        // 1. Process Categories sheet first
        if (workbook.SheetNames.includes('Categories')) {
            const wsCategories = workbook.Sheets['Categories'];
            const categoryData = xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(wsCategories) as any[];

            for (const row of categoryData) {
                const categoryName = row['Category Name']?.toString()?.trim();
                if (categoryName) {
                    await prisma.category.upsert({
                        where: { name: categoryName },
                        update: {},
                        create: { name: categoryName },
                    });
                    categoriesUpserted++;
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Process Products sheet
        if (workbook.SheetNames.includes('Products')) {
            const wsProducts = workbook.Sheets['Products'];
            const productData = xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(wsProducts) as any[];

            for (const row of productData) {
                const barcode = row['Barcode']?.toString()?.trim();
                const productName = row['Product Name']?.toString()?.trim();
                const description = row['Description']?.toString()?.trim() || '';
                const basePriceVal = parseFloat(row['Price']) || 0;
                const costPriceVal = parseFloat(row['Cost Price']) || 0;
                const retailPriceVal = parseFloat(row['Retail Price']) || basePriceVal;
                const taxRateVal = parseFloat(row['Tax Rate']) || 0;
                const taxInclusiveVal = row['Tax Inclusive']?.toString()?.trim()?.toLowerCase() === 'yes';
                const stockQtyVal = parseInt(row['Stock Quantity'], 10);
                const categoryName = row['Category Name']?.toString()?.trim();
                const serialNumbersStr = row['Serial Numbers']?.toString()?.trim() || '';

                // Row validation: Skip rows missing essential values
                if (!barcode || !productName || !categoryName) {
                    continue;
                }

                // Ensure category exists, create dynamically if not
                let category = await prisma.category.findUnique({
                    where: { name: categoryName },
                });
                if (!category) {
                    category = await prisma.category.create({
                        data: { name: categoryName },
                    });
                    categoriesUpserted++;
                }

                // Check if product with this barcode already exists
                let product = await prisma.product.findUnique({
                    where: { barcode },
                });

                const parsedSns = serialNumbersStr
                    ? serialNumbersStr.split(',').map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
                    : [];

                const targetQty = Math.max(isNaN(stockQtyVal) ? 0 : stockQtyVal, parsedSns.length);

                const dataPayload = {
                    name: productName,
                    description,
                    price: basePriceVal || retailPriceVal,
                    costPrice: costPriceVal,
                    retailPrice: retailPriceVal,
                    taxRate: taxRateVal,
                    taxInclusive: taxInclusiveVal,
                    stockQty: targetQty,
                    categoryId: category.id,
                };

                if (product) {
                    // Update existing product properties
                    product = await prisma.product.update({
                        where: { barcode },
                        data: dataPayload,
                    });
                    productsUpdated++;
                } else {
                    // Create new product
                    product = await prisma.product.create({
                        data: {
                            ...dataPayload,
                            barcode,
                        },
                    });
                    productsCreated++;
                }

                // Sync Serial Numbers
                const prefix = productName.substring(0, 3).toUpperCase();
                const existingStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: product.id } });
                const existingSns = existingStock.map((s: any) => s.serialNumber);

                // Delete AVAILABLE stock items that are no longer in the imported serial numbers list
                if (parsedSns.length > 0) {
                    const toDelete = existingStock.filter(
                        (s: any) => !parsedSns.includes(s.serialNumber) && s.status === 'AVAILABLE'
                    );
                    if (toDelete.length > 0) {
                        await (prisma as any).stockItem.deleteMany({
                            where: { id: { in: toDelete.map((s: any) => s.id) } },
                        });
                    }

                    // Add new ones
                    for (const sn of parsedSns) {
                        if (!existingSns.includes(sn)) {
                            const globalExisting = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findUnique({ where: { serialNumber: sn } });
                            if (!globalExisting) {
                                await (prisma as any).stockItem.create({
                                    data: {
                                        productId: product.id,
                                        serialNumber: sn,
                                        status: 'AVAILABLE',
                                    },
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Auto-generate missing serial numbers to match targetQty
                let updatedStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: product.id } });
                let currentAvailable = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;

                if (targetQty > currentAvailable) {
                    const diff = targetQty - currentAvailable;
                    for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {
                        const randomSn = `${prefix}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}-${Math.floor(
                            1000 + Math.random() * 9000
                        )}`;
                        await (prisma as any).stockItem.create({
                            data: {
                                productId: product.id,
                                serialNumber: randomSn,
                                status: 'AVAILABLE',
                            },
                        });
                    }
                } else if (parsedSns.length === 0 && targetQty < currentAvailable) {
                    // Prune extra AVAILABLE stock items if no explicit S/Ns were supplied but count is lowered
                    const diff = currentAvailable - targetQty;
                    const availableItems = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE');
                    const toDelete = availableItems.slice(0, diff);
                    await (prisma as any).stockItem.deleteMany({
                        where: { id: { in: toDelete.map((s: any) => s.id) } },
                    });
                }

                // Re-sync final stock quantity count
                updatedStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: product.id } });
                const finalQty = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;
                await prisma.product.update({
                    where: { id: product.id },
                    data: { stockQty: finalQty },
                });
            }
        }

        res.json({
            success: true,
            summary: {
                categoriesUpserted,
                productsCreated,
                productsUpdated,
            },
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Failed to import Excel file:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to parse Excel file.' });
    }
});

export default router;

FILE: server\src\routes\inventoryAudits.ts
================================================================================

import express from "express";
import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";
import { authenticate } from "../middleware/auth";

const router = express.Router();
const prisma = new PrismaClient();

// Get all inventory audits
router.get("/", authenticate, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const audits = await prisma.inventoryAudit.findMany({
            orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
            include: {
                auditor: { select: { id: true, name: true } },
                items: {
                    include: {
                        product: { select: { name: true, barcode: true } }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
        res.json(audits);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to fetch inventory audits:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to fetch inventory audits" });
    }
});

// Create a new inventory audit session
router.post("/", authenticate, async (req: any, res) => {
    try {
        const { notes, items } = req.body;
        // items is array of { productId, actualQty, notes }

        const newAudit = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
            // Fetch current stock for all products in audit
            const productIds = items.map((i: any) => i.productId);
            const products = await tx.product.findMany({
                where: { id: { in: productIds } },
                select: { id: true, stockQty: true }
            });

            const productMap = new Map(products.map(p => [p.id, p.stockQty]));

            const auditItems = items.map((i: any) => {
                const expectedQty = productMap.get(i.productId) || 0;
                return {
                    productId: i.productId,
                    expectedQty,
                    actualQty: i.actualQty,
                    variance: i.actualQty - expectedQty,
                    notes: i.notes || ""
                };
            });

            // Create audit
            const audit = await tx.inventoryAudit.create({
                data: {
                    auditorId: req.user.id,
                    notes: notes || "",
                    items: {
                        create: auditItems
                    }
                },
                include: { items: true }
            });

            return audit;
        });

        // Also add to audit logs
        await prisma.auditLog.create({
            data: {
                userId: req.user.id,
                action: "CREATE",
                entity: "InventoryAudit",
                entityId: newAudit.id,
                details: JSON.stringify({ itemsCount: newAudit.items.length })
            }
        });

        res.status(201).json(newAudit);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to create inventory audit:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to create inventory audit" });
    }
});

// Complete an audit and apply variances to stock
router.post("/:id/complete", authenticate, async (req: any, res) => {
    try {
        const auditId = parseInt(req.params.id);
        const audit = await prisma.inventoryAudit.findUnique({
            where: { id: auditId },
            include: { items: true }
        });

        if (!audit) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Audit not found" });
        if (audit.status === "COMPLETED") return res.status(400).json({ error: "Audit already completed" });

        await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
            // Update stock for each item based on actualQty
            for (const item of audit.items) {
                if (item.variance !== 0) {
                    await tx.product.update({
                        where: { id: item.productId },
                        data: { stockQty: item.actualQty }
                    });
                }
            }

            // Mark audit as complete
            await tx.inventoryAudit.update({
                where: { id: auditId },
                data: { status: "COMPLETED", completedAt: new Date() }
            });
        });
        
        await prisma.auditLog.create({
            data: {
                userId: req.user.id,
                action: "UPDATE",
                entity: "InventoryAudit",
                entityId: auditId,
                details: JSON.stringify({ action: "COMPLETED" })
            }
        });

        res.json({ message: "Audit completed successfully" });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to complete inventory audit:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Failed to complete inventory audit" });
    }
});

export default router;

FILE: server\src\routes\paymentModes.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// GET all payment modes
router.get('/', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const activeOnly = req.query.active === 'true' || req.user!.role === 'CASHIER';
        const where = activeOnly ? { isActive: true } : {};

        const modes = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findMany({
            where,
            orderBy: { name: 'asc' },
        });
        res.json(modes);
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Get payment modes error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to fetch payment modes.' });
    }
});

// POST create payment mode (ADMIN or STAFF only)
router.post('/', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { name, description, isActive } = req.body;

        if (!name || !name.trim()) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name is required.' });
            return;
        }

        // Check uniqueness
        const existing = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findUnique({
            where: { name: name.trim() },
        });
        if (existing) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'A payment mode with this name already exists.' });
            return;
        }

        const mode = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.create({
            data: {
                name: name.trim(),
                description: description || '',
                isActive: isActive !== undefined ? Boolean(isActive) : true,
            },
        });

        res.status(201).json(mode);
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Create payment mode error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to create payment mode.' });
    }
});

// PUT update payment mode (ADMIN or STAFF only)
router.put('/:id', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const id = Number(req.params.id);
        const { name, description, isActive } = req.body;

        if (isNaN(id)) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid ID.' });
            return;
        }

        const existingMode = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findUnique({ where: { id } });
        if (!existingMode) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Payment mode not found.' });
            return;
        }

        const data: any = {};
        if (name !== undefined) {
            if (!name || !name.trim()) {
                res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name cannot be empty.' });
                return;
            }
            // Check uniqueness if name changed
            if (name.trim().toLowerCase() !== existingMode.name.toLowerCase()) {
                const dup = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findUnique({ where: { name: name.trim() } });
                if (dup) {
                    res.status(400).json({ error: 'A payment mode with this name already exists.' });
                    return;
                }
            }
            data.name = name.trim();
        }

        if (description !== undefined) {
            data.description = description || '';
        }

        if (isActive !== undefined) {
            data.isActive = Boolean(isActive);
        }

        const mode = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.update({
            where: { id },
            data,
        });

        res.json(mode);
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Update payment mode error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to update payment mode.' });
    }
});

// DELETE payment mode (ADMIN or STAFF only)
router.delete('/:id', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const id = Number(req.params.id);

        if (isNaN(id)) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid ID.' });
            return;
        }

        const existingMode = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findUnique({ where: { id } });
        if (!existingMode) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Payment mode not found.' });
            return;
        }

        await (prisma as any).paymentMode.delete({ where: { id } });
        res.json({ success: true, message: 'Payment mode deleted successfully.' });
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Delete payment mode error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to delete payment mode.' });
    }
});

export default router;

FILE: server\src\routes\products.ts

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';
import { upload } from '../middleware/upload';
import { generateBarcodeString, generateBarcodeImage } from '../lib/barcode';

const router = Router();

// GET all products
router.get('/', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const products = await prisma.product.findMany({
            include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
        });
        res.json(products);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get products error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch products.' });
    }
});

// GET search products
router.get('/search', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { q, categoryId, barcode } = req.query;

        const where: any = {};
        if (q) {
            where.OR = [
                { name: { contains: String(q) } },
                { description: { contains: String(q) } },
                { barcode: { contains: String(q) } },
            ];
        }
        if (categoryId) {
            where.categoryId = Number(categoryId);
        }
        if (barcode) {
            where.barcode = String(barcode);
        }

        const products = await prisma.product.findMany({
            where,
            include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
        });
        res.json(products);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Search products error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to search products.' });
    }
});

// GET single product
router.get('/:id', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const product = await prisma.product.findUnique({
            where: { id: Number(req.params.id) },
            include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
        });
        if (!product) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Product not found.' });
            return;
        }
        res.json(product);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get product error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch product.' });
    }
});

// POST create product
router.post(
    '/',
    authenticate,
    authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'),
    upload.single('image'),
    async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
        try {
            const { name, description, price, retailPrice, costPrice, taxRate, taxInclusive, stockQty, categoryId, barcode, serialNumbers } = req.body;

            const finalBarcode = barcode || generateBarcodeString();

            // Check barcode uniqueness
            const existing = await prisma.product.findUnique({ where: { barcode: finalBarcode } });
            if (existing) {
                res.status(400).json({ error: 'Barcode already exists.' });
                return;
            }

            // Generate barcode image
            const barcodeImageUrl = await generateBarcodeImage(finalBarcode);

            const imageUrl = req.file ? `/uploads/products/${req.file.filename}` : null;

            // Parse serial numbers
            let parsedSns: string[] = [];
            if (serialNumbers) {
                parsedSns = typeof serialNumbers === 'string'
                    ? serialNumbers.split(/[,\n]/).map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
                    : serialNumbers;
            }

            const hasSns = parsedSns.length > 0;
            const targetQty = hasSns ? parsedSns.length : (parseInt(stockQty) || 0);

            // Auto-generate missing S/Ns only if no manual S/Ns were entered
            const finalSns = [...parsedSns];
            if (!hasSns && targetQty > 0) {
                const prefix = (name || 'PROD').substring(0, 3).toUpperCase();
                for (let i = 0; i < targetQty; i++) {
                    const randomSn = `${prefix}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
                    finalSns.push(randomSn);
                }
            }

            const product = await prisma.product.create({
                data: {
                    name,
                    description: description || '',
                    price: parseFloat(price) || 0,
                    retailPrice: parseFloat(retailPrice) || 0,
                    costPrice: parseFloat(costPrice) || 0,
                    taxRate: parseFloat(taxRate) || 0,
                    taxInclusive: taxInclusive === 'true' || taxInclusive === true,
                    barcode: finalBarcode,
                    imageUrl,
                    stockQty: targetQty,
                    categoryId: parseInt(categoryId),
                    stockItems: {
                        create: finalSns.map(sn => ({
                            serialNumber: sn,
                            status: 'AVAILABLE'
                        }))
                    }
                } as any,
                include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
            });

            res.status(201).json({ ...product, barcodeImageUrl });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Create product error:', error);
            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to create product.' });
        }
    }
);

// PUT update product
router.put(
    '/:id',
    authenticate,
    authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'),
    upload.single('image'),
    async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
        try {
            const id = Number(req.params.id);
            const { name, description, price, retailPrice, costPrice, taxRate, taxInclusive, stockQty, categoryId, serialNumbers } = req.body;

            const data: any = {};
            if (name !== undefined) data.name = name;
            if (description !== undefined) data.description = description;
            if (price !== undefined) data.price = parseFloat(price);
            if (retailPrice !== undefined) data.retailPrice = parseFloat(retailPrice);
            if (costPrice !== undefined) data.costPrice = parseFloat(costPrice);
            if (taxRate !== undefined) data.taxRate = parseFloat(taxRate);
            if (taxInclusive !== undefined) data.taxInclusive = taxInclusive === 'true' || taxInclusive === true;
            if (categoryId !== undefined) data.categoryId = parseInt(categoryId);
            if (req.file) {
                data.imageUrl = `/uploads/products/${req.file.filename}`;
            } else if (req.body.removeImage === 'true') {
                data.imageUrl = null;
            }

            // Update standard product fields
            let product = await prisma.product.update({
                where: { id },
                data,
                include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
            });

            const prodName = name || product.name;
            const prefix = prodName.substring(0, 3).toUpperCase();

            // Handle Serial Numbers sync
            if (serialNumbers !== undefined) {
                const parsedSns: string[] = typeof serialNumbers === 'string'
                    ? serialNumbers.split(/[,\n]/).map((s: string) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
                    : (Array.isArray(serialNumbers) ? serialNumbers.map((s: any) => String(s).trim()) : []);

                const hasSns = parsedSns.length > 0;

                const existingStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: id } });
                const existingSns = existingStock.map((s: any) => s.serialNumber);

                // Delete AVAILABLE stock items that are no longer in the list
                const snsToDelete = existingStock.filter((s: any) => !parsedSns.includes(s.serialNumber) && s.status === 'AVAILABLE');
                if (snsToDelete.length > 0) {
                    await (prisma as any).stockItem.deleteMany({
                        where: { id: { in: snsToDelete.map((s: any) => s.id) } }
                    });
                }

                // Add new serial numbers
                const snsToAdd = parsedSns.filter(sn => !existingSns.includes(sn));
                for (const sn of snsToAdd) {
                    const globalExisting = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findUnique({ where: { serialNumber: sn } });
                    if (!globalExisting) {
                        await (prisma as any).stockItem.create({
                            data: {
                                productId: id,
                                serialNumber: sn,
                                status: 'AVAILABLE'
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }

                // If stockQty (target) is larger than current AVAILABLE, generate the difference (only if no manual S/Ns are present)
                let updatedStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: id } });
                let currentAvailableCount = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;
                const targetQty = parseInt(stockQty);

                if (!hasSns && !isNaN(targetQty) && targetQty > currentAvailableCount) {
                    const diff = targetQty - currentAvailableCount;
                    for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {
                        const randomSn = `${prefix}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
                        await (prisma as any).stockItem.create({
                            data: {
                                productId: id,
                                serialNumber: randomSn,
                                status: 'AVAILABLE'
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }

                // Sync the actual available quantity in the DB to the product stockQty
                updatedStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: id } });
                const totalStock = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;
                product = await prisma.product.update({
                    where: { id },
                    data: { stockQty: totalStock },
                    include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any
                });
            } else if (stockQty !== undefined) {
                // If serialNumbers is not provided, adjust the auto-generated ones according to stockQty
                const targetQty = parseInt(stockQty) || 0;
                const existingStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: id } });
                const currentAvailable = existingStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE');

                if (targetQty > currentAvailable.length) {
                    const diff = targetQty - currentAvailable.length;
                    for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {
                        const randomSn = `${prefix}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
                        await (prisma as any).stockItem.create({
                            data: {
                                productId: id,
                                serialNumber: randomSn,
                                status: 'AVAILABLE'
                            }
                        });
                    }
                } else if (targetQty < currentAvailable.length) {
                    const diff = currentAvailable.length - targetQty;
                    const toDelete = currentAvailable.slice(0, diff);
                    await (prisma as any).stockItem.deleteMany({
                        where: { id: { in: toDelete.map((s: any) => s.id) } }
                    });
                }

                const updatedStock = await (prisma as any).stockItem.findMany({ where: { productId: id } });
                const totalStock = updatedStock.filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE').length;
                product = await prisma.product.update({
                    where: { id },
                    data: { stockQty: totalStock },
                    include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any
                });
            }

            res.json(product);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Update product error:', error);
            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to update product.' });
        }
    }
);

// DELETE product
router.delete(
    '/:id',
    authenticate,
    authorize('ADMIN'),
    async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
        try {
            await prisma.product.delete({ where: { id: Number(req.params.id) } });
            res.json({ message: 'Product deleted successfully.' });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Delete product error:', error);
            res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to delete product.' });
        }
    }
);

// GET barcode image for a product
router.get('/:id/barcode', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const product = await prisma.product.findUnique({ where: { id: Number(req.params.id) } });
        if (!product) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Product not found.' });
            return;
        }
        const barcodeImageUrl = await generateBarcodeImage(product.barcode);
        res.json({ barcode: product.barcode, barcodeImageUrl });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Barcode error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to generate barcode.' });
    }
});

export default router;


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\routes\settings.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import multer from 'multer';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// Configure upload directory for branding images
const BRANDING_UPLOAD_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '../../uploads/branding');
if (!fs.existsSync(BRANDING_UPLOAD_DIR)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(BRANDING_UPLOAD_DIR, { recursive: true });
}

// Multer Storage Configuration
const storage = multer.diskStorage({
    destination: (_req, _file, cb) => {
        cb(null, BRANDING_UPLOAD_DIR);
    },
    filename: (_req, file, cb) => {
        const uniqueSuffix = Date.now() + '-' + Math.round(Math.random() * 1e9);
        cb(null, 'logo-' + uniqueSuffix + path.extname(file.originalname));
    },
});

// File filter (only allow web images)
const fileFilter = (_req: any, file: Express.Multer.File, cb: multer.FileFilterCallback) => {
    const allowedTypes = /jpeg|jpg|png|gif|webp|svg/;
    const ext = allowedTypes.test(path.extname(file.originalname).toLowerCase());
    const mime = allowedTypes.test(file.mimetype);
    if (ext && mime) {
        cb(null, true);
    } else {
        cb(new Error('Only image files are allowed'));
    }
};

const upload = multer({
    storage,
    fileFilter,
    limits: { fileSize: 5 * 1024 * 1024 }, // 5MB limit
});

// GET current settings (Public - so login page can access it)
router.get('/', async (_req, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        let settings = await prisma.settings.findUnique({
            where: { id: 1 },
        });

        // Fallback in case seed didn't run or table is empty
        if (!settings) {
            settings = await prisma.settings.create({
                data: {
                    id: 1,
                    appName: 'Shadow Apollo',
                    subtitle: 'Inventory & POS System',
                    logoIcon: '⚡',
                    logoUrl: null,
                    posPin: null,
                    posPinLength: 4,
                    emailDomain: 'shadow.com',
                    maintenanceMode: false,
                },
            });
        }

        // Return a safe settings object without exposing the raw PIN
        const safeSettings = {
            id: settings.id,
            appName: settings.appName,
            subtitle: settings.subtitle,
            logoIcon: settings.logoIcon,
            logoUrl: settings.logoUrl,
            posPinLength: settings.posPinLength,
            hasPosPin: !!settings.posPin,
            emailDomain: settings.emailDomain,
            maintenanceMode: settings.maintenanceMode,
        };

        res.json(safeSettings);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to get settings:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch settings' });
    }
});

// PUT update settings (Protected - ADMIN and STAFF only)
router.put('/', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), upload.single('logo'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { appName, subtitle, logoIcon, removeLogo, posPin, posPinLength, emailDomain, maintenanceMode } = req.body;

        if (!appName || !subtitle || !logoIcon) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'appName, subtitle, and logoIcon are required.' });
            return;
        }

        // Determine if logoUrl should be updated
        let logoUrlUpdate: string | null | undefined = undefined;
        if (req.file) {
            logoUrlUpdate = `/uploads/branding/${req.file.filename}`;
        } else if (removeLogo === 'true') {
            logoUrlUpdate = null;
        }

        const resolvedPosPin = posPin === '' ? null : posPin;
        const isMaintenance = maintenanceMode !== undefined
            ? (maintenanceMode === 'true' || maintenanceMode === true)
            : undefined;

        const settings = await prisma.settings.upsert({
            where: { id: 1 },
            update: {
                appName,
                subtitle,
                logoIcon,
                ...(logoUrlUpdate !== undefined ? { logoUrl: logoUrlUpdate } : {}),
                ...(posPin !== undefined ? { posPin: resolvedPosPin } : {}),
                ...(posPinLength !== undefined ? { posPinLength: Number(posPinLength) } : {}),
                ...(emailDomain !== undefined ? { emailDomain } : {}),
                ...(isMaintenance !== undefined ? { maintenanceMode: isMaintenance } : {}),
            },
            create: {
                id: 1,
                appName,
                subtitle,
                logoIcon,
                logoUrl: logoUrlUpdate || null,
                posPin: resolvedPosPin || null,
                posPinLength: posPinLength ? Number(posPinLength) : 4,
                emailDomain: emailDomain || 'shadow.com',
                maintenanceMode: isMaintenance || false,
            },
        });

        // Return safe settings
        res.json({
            id: settings.id,
            appName: settings.appName,
            subtitle: settings.subtitle,
            logoIcon: settings.logoIcon,
            logoUrl: settings.logoUrl,
            posPinLength: settings.posPinLength,
            hasPosPin: !!settings.posPin,
            emailDomain: settings.emailDomain,
            maintenanceMode: settings.maintenanceMode,
        });
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error('Failed to update settings:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: error.message || 'Failed to update settings' });
    }
});

// POST verify admin password or POS PIN (Protected - any logged in cashier can hit this to verify void permission)
router.post('/verify-pin', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { value } = req.body; // EITHER the 4/6-digit PIN OR the admin password
        if (!value) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Verification value is required.' });
            return;
        }

        // 1. Check against the POS PIN setting
        const settings = await prisma.settings.findUnique({ where: { id: 1 } });
        if (settings && settings.posPin && settings.posPin === String(value)) {
            res.json({ valid: true });
            return;
        }

        // 2. Check against the Admin password(s)
        const admins = await prisma.user.findMany({ where: { role: 'ADMIN' } });
        const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');
        for (const admin of admins) {
            const match = await bcrypt.compare(value, admin.password);
            if (match) {
                res.json({ valid: true });
                return;
            }
        }

        res.json({ valid: false });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Verify pin error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to verify admin credentials.' });
    }
});

export default router;


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\routes\transactions.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// POST create transaction (POS checkout)
router.post('/', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'CASHIER'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        // Check if system is in maintenance mode
        const settings = await prisma.settings.findUnique({ where: { id: 1 } });
        if (settings?.maintenanceMode) {
            res.status(403).json({ error: 'Checkout is disabled. System is under maintenance (Price Check Mode Only).' });
            return;
        }

        const { items, discount, paymentModeId, amountTendered, changeAmount } = req.body;

        if (!items || !Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Transaction must have at least one item.' });
            return;
        }

        // Calculate total and validate stock
        let totalAmount = 0;
        let totalTaxAmount = 0;
        const itemsData: Array<{
            productId: number;
            quantity: number;
            price: number;
            subtotal: number;
            taxAmount: number;
            serialNumbers: string[];
        }> = [];

        for (const item of items) {
            const product = (await prisma.product.findUnique({
                where: { id: item.productId },
                include: { stockItems: true } as any
            })) as any;

            if (!product) {
                res.status(400).json({ error: `Product ID ${item.productId} not found.` });
                return;
            }

            if (product.stockQty < item.quantity) {
                res.status(400).json({ error: `Insufficient stock for ${product.name}. Available: ${product.stockQty}` });
                return;
            }

            // Determine serial numbers to sell
            const snsToSell: string[] = item.serialNumbers || [];
            if (snsToSell.length > 0) {
                const availableSns = (product.stockItems || []).filter(
                    (s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE' && snsToSell.includes(s.serialNumber)
                );
                if (availableSns.length !== snsToSell.length) {
                    res.status(400).json({
                        error: `One or more serial numbers for "${product.name}" are not available.`,
                    });
                    return;
                }
            } else {
                // Auto-pick first available
                const availableSns = (product.stockItems || []).filter((s: any) => s.status === 'AVAILABLE');
                if (availableSns.length < item.quantity) {
                    res.status(400).json({ error: `Insufficient serialized stock for ${product.name}.` });
                    return;
                }
                snsToSell.push(...availableSns.slice(0, item.quantity).map((s: any) => s.serialNumber));
            }

            // Calculate price & margin tax
            const unitRetail = product.retailPrice > 0 ? product.retailPrice : product.price;
            const taxRate = product.taxRate;

            let unitTax = 0;
            let unitSellingPrice = unitRetail;

            if (taxRate > 0) {
                if (product.taxInclusive) {
                    // VAT Inclusive: tax is part of the total price
                    const netBase = unitRetail / (1 + taxRate / 100);
                    unitTax = unitRetail - netBase;
                    unitSellingPrice = unitRetail;
                } else {
                    // VAT Exclusive: tax is added on top of base retail price
                    unitTax = unitRetail * (taxRate / 100);
                    unitSellingPrice = unitRetail + unitTax;
                }
            }

            const itemSubtotal = unitSellingPrice * item.quantity;
            const itemTaxTotal = unitTax * item.quantity;

            totalAmount += itemSubtotal;
            totalTaxAmount += itemTaxTotal;

            itemsData.push({
                productId: product.id,
                quantity: item.quantity,
                price: unitSellingPrice,
                subtotal: itemSubtotal,
                taxAmount: itemTaxTotal,
                serialNumbers: snsToSell,
            });
        }

        const discountAmount = discount || 0;
        totalAmount = totalAmount - discountAmount;

        // Resolve payment mode name
        let resolvedPaymentModeName: string | null = null;
        if (paymentModeId) {
            const pm = await (prisma as any).paymentMode.findUnique({
                where: { id: Number(paymentModeId) }
            });
            if (pm) {
                resolvedPaymentModeName = pm.name;
            }
        }

        // Create transaction and deduct stock in a transaction
        const transaction = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx: any) => {
            const txn = await tx.transaction.create({
                data: {
                    cashierId: req.user!.id,
                    totalAmount,
                    taxAmount: totalTaxAmount,
                    discount: discountAmount,
                    paymentModeId: paymentModeId ? Number(paymentModeId) : null,
                    paymentModeName: resolvedPaymentModeName,
                    amountTendered: amountTendered !== undefined ? Number(amountTendered) : null,
                    changeAmount: changeAmount !== undefined ? Number(changeAmount) : null,
                    items: {
                        create: itemsData.map((i) => ({
                            productId: i.productId,
                            quantity: i.quantity,
                            price: i.price,
                            subtotal: i.subtotal,
                            taxAmount: i.taxAmount,
                        })),
                    },
                } as any,
                include: {
                    items: { include: { product: true, stockItems: true } as any },
                    cashier: { select: { name: true, role: true } },
                    paymentMode: true,
                } as any,
            });

            // Mark stock items as SOLD and link to their transaction items
            for (const item of (txn.items || [])) {
                const matchedItemData = itemsData.find((id) => id.productId === item.productId);
                if (matchedItemData && matchedItemData.serialNumbers.length > 0) {
                    await tx.stockItem.updateMany({
                        where: {
                            serialNumber: { in: matchedItemData.serialNumbers },
                        },
                        data: {
                            status: 'SOLD',
                            transactionItemId: item.id,
                        },
                    });
                }
            }

            // Deduct stock qty on products
            for (const item of itemsData) {
                await tx.product.update({
                    where: { id: item.productId },
                    data: { stockQty: { decrement: item.quantity } },
                });
            }

            // Re-fetch to get updated relations (like newly linked stockItems)
            const finalTxn = await tx.transaction.findUnique({
                where: { id: txn.id },
                include: {
                    items: { include: { product: true, stockItems: true } as any },
                    cashier: { select: { name: true, role: true } },
                    paymentMode: true,
                } as any,
            });
            return finalTxn;
        });

        res.status(201).json(transaction);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Create transaction error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to create transaction.' });
    }
});

// GET all transactions
router.get('/', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { startDate, endDate, cashierId } = req.query;
        const where: any = {};

        if (startDate && endDate) {
            where.createdAt = {
                gte: new Date(String(startDate)),
                lte: new Date(String(endDate)),
            };
        }

        if (cashierId) {
            where.cashierId = Number(cashierId);
        }

        // Non-admin cashiers can only see their own transactions
        if (req.user!.role === 'CASHIER') {
            where.cashierId = req.user!.id;
        }

        const transactions = await prisma.transaction.findMany({
            where,
            include: {
                items: { include: { product: true, stockItems: true } as any },
                cashier: { select: { name: true, email: true, role: true } },
                paymentMode: true,
            } as any,
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
        });
        res.json(transactions);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get transactions error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch transactions.' });
    }
});

// GET dashboard stats
router.get('/stats/dashboard', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const totalProducts = await prisma.product.count();
        const lowStockProducts = await prisma.product.count({ where: { stockQty: { lte: 10 } } });
        const totalTransactions = await prisma.transaction.count({ where: { status: { not: 'VOIDED' } } });
        const totalCategories = await prisma.category.count();

        // Today's sales
        const today = new Date();
        today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
        const todaySales = await prisma.transaction.aggregate({
            where: { createdAt: { gte: today }, status: { not: 'VOIDED' } },
            _sum: { totalAmount: true },
            _count: true,
        });

        // Recent transactions
        const recentTransactions = await prisma.transaction.findMany({
            take: 5,
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
            include: {
                cashier: { select: { name: true } },
                _count: { select: { items: true } },
            },
        });

        res.json({
            totalProducts,
            lowStockProducts,
            totalTransactions,
            totalCategories,
            todaySalesAmount: todaySales._sum.totalAmount || 0,
            todaySalesCount: todaySales._count,
            recentTransactions,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Dashboard stats error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch dashboard stats.' });
    }
});

// GET stats for charts (ADMIN only)
router.get('/stats/charts', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const sevenDaysAgo = new Date();
        sevenDaysAgo.setDate(sevenDaysAgo.getDate() - 7);
        sevenDaysAgo.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

        const transactions = await prisma.transaction.findMany({
            where: { createdAt: { gte: sevenDaysAgo }, status: { not: 'VOIDED' } },
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' },
        });

        const salesByDay: { [key: string]: { date: string; amount: number; count: number } } = {};
        for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
            const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
            const key = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
            salesByDay[key] = { date: dateStr, amount: 0, count: 0 };
        }

        transactions.forEach(t => {
            const key = new Date(t.createdAt).toISOString().split('T')[0];
            if (salesByDay[key]) {
                salesByDay[key].amount += t.totalAmount;
                salesByDay[key].count += 1;
            }
        });

        const salesOverTime = Object.keys(salesByDay).sort().map(key => salesByDay[key]);

        const categories = await prisma.category.findMany({
            include: {
                products: {
                    select: { stockQty: true }
                }
            }
        });

        const stockByCategory = categories.map(cat => {
            const totalStock = cat.products.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.stockQty, 0);
            return {
                categoryName: cat.name,
                stockQty: totalStock
            };
        });

        res.json({
            salesOverTime,
            stockByCategory
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Charts stats error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch charts stats.' });
    }
});

// GET single transaction
router.get('/:id', authenticate, async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const transaction = await prisma.transaction.findUnique({
            where: { id: Number(req.params.id) },
            include: {
                items: { include: { product: true, stockItems: true } as any },
                cashier: { select: { name: true, email: true, role: true } },
                paymentMode: true,
            } as any,
        });
        if (!transaction) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Transaction not found.' });
            return;
        }
        res.json(transaction);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get transaction error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch transaction.' });
    }
});

// POST void transaction
router.post('/:id/void', authenticate, authorize('ADMIN', 'STAFF'), async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const id = Number(req.params.id);
        const transaction = await prisma.transaction.findUnique({
            where: { id },
            include: {
                items: { include: { stockItems: true } as any }
            } as any
        }) as any;

        if (!transaction) {
            res.status(404).json({ error: 'Transaction not found.' });
            return;
        }

        if (transaction.status === 'VOIDED') {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Transaction is already voided.' });
            return;
        }

        // Revert in a database transaction
        await prisma.$transaction(async (tx: any) => {
            // 1. Update transaction status
            await tx.transaction.update({
                where: { id },
                data: { status: 'VOIDED' }
            });

            // 2. Revert each item's stock qty and stock item statuses
            for (const item of (transaction.items || [])) {
                // Return stockItems back to AVAILABLE and unlink
                await tx.stockItem.updateMany({
                    where: { transactionItemId: item.id },
                    data: {
                        status: 'AVAILABLE',
                        transactionItemId: null
                    }
                });

                // Increment product stockQty
                await tx.product.update({
                    where: { id: item.productId },
                    data: { stockQty: { increment: item.quantity } }
                });
            }
        });

        res.json({ message: 'Transaction voided successfully.' });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Void transaction error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to void transaction.' });
    }
});

export default router;


================================================================================
FILE: server\src\routes\users.ts
================================================================================

import { Router, Response } from 'express';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import prisma from '../lib/prisma';
import { AuthRequest, authenticate, authorize } from '../middleware/auth';

const router = Router();

// All user routes require ADMIN role
router.use(authenticate, authorize('ADMIN'));

// GET all users
router.get('/', async (_req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const users = await prisma.user.findMany({
            select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true, createdAt: true },
            orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
        });
        res.json(users);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Get users error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to fetch users.' });
    }
});

// POST create user
router.post('/', async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const { name, email, password, role } = req.body;

        if (!name || !email || !password) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name, email, and password are required.' });
            return;
        }

        const validRoles = ['ADMIN', 'STAFF', 'CASHIER'];
        if (role && !validRoles.includes(role)) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid role. Must be ADMIN, STAFF, or CASHIER.' });
            return;
        }

        const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
        if (existing) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email already exists.' });
            return;
        }

        const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);

        const user = await prisma.user.create({
            data: { name, email, password: hashedPassword, role: role || 'CASHIER' },
            select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true, createdAt: true },
        });

        res.status(201).json(user);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Create user error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to create user.' });
    }
});

// PUT update user
router.put('/:id', async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const id = Number(req.params.id);
        const { name, email, password, role } = req.body;

        const data: any = {};
        if (name) data.name = name;
        if (email) data.email = email;
        if (role) data.role = role;
        if (password) data.password = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10);

        const user = await prisma.user.update({
            where: { id },
            data,
            select: { id: true, name: true, email: true, role: true, createdAt: true },
        });

        res.json(user);
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Update user error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to update user.' });
    }
});

// DELETE user
router.delete('/:id', async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response): Promise<void> => {
    try {
        const id = Number(req.params.id);

        // Prevent deleting yourself
        if (req.user!.id === id) {
            res.status(400).json({ error: 'You cannot delete your own account.' });
            return;
        }

        await prisma.user.delete({ where: { id } });
        res.json({ message: 'User deleted successfully.' });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Delete user error:', error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: 'Failed to delete user.' });
    }
});

export default router;

import prisma from './lib/prisma';

async function test() {
  try {
    console.log('Testing settings connection...');
    const settings = await prisma.settings.findUnique({
      where: { id: 1 },
    });
    console.log('Settings read successfully:', settings);

    console.log('Testing categories connection...');
    const categories = await prisma.category.findMany({
        include: { _count: { select: { products: true } } },
        orderBy: { name: 'asc' },
    });
    console.log('Categories read successfully, count:', categories.length);

    console.log('Testing products search/findMany connection...');
    const products = await prisma.product.findMany({
        include: { category: true, stockItems: true } as any,
        orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
    });
    console.log('Products read successfully:', JSON.stringify(products, null, 2));

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error connecting to database:', error);
  } finally {
    await prisma.$disconnect();
  }
}

test();

import http from 'http';

function makeRequest() {
  console.log('Sending request to server...');
  http.get('http://localhost:5174/api/settings', (res) => {
    console.log('STATUS:', res.statusCode);
    console.log('HEADERS:', JSON.stringify(res.headers));
    res.setEncoding('utf8');
    let rawData = '';
    res.on('data', (chunk) => { rawData += chunk; });
    res.on('end', () => {
      try {
        console.log('BODY:', rawData);
      } catch (e: any) {
        console.error(e.message);
      }
    });
  }).on('error', (e) => {
    console.error(`Got error: ${e.message}`);
  });
}

makeRequest();

import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";

const prisma = new PrismaClient();

export const logAudit = async (
    userId: number,
    action: string,
    entity: string,
    entityId?: number,
    details?: any
) => {
    try {
        await prisma.auditLog.create({
            data: {
                userId,
                action,
                entity,
                entityId,
                details: details ? JSON.stringify(details) : null
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to log audit:", error);
    }
};


================================================================================
FILE: server\prisma\schema.prisma
================================================================================

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "sqlite"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

model User {
  id           Int           @id @default(autoincrement())
  name         String
  email        String        @unique
  password     String
  role         String        @default("CASHIER")
  createdAt      DateTime         @default(now())
  transactions   Transaction[]
  auditLogs      AuditLog[]
  inventoryAudits InventoryAudit[]
}

model Category {
  id       Int       @id @default(autoincrement())
  name     String    @unique
  products Product[]
}

model Product {
  id               Int               @id @default(autoincrement())
  name             String
  description      String            @default("")
  price            Float             // Keep standard price as fallback or base price
  retailPrice      Float             @default(0)
  costPrice        Float             @default(0)
  taxRate          Float             @default(0)
  taxInclusive     Boolean           @default(false)
  barcode          String            @unique
  imageUrl         String?
  stockQty         Int               @default(0)
  categoryId       Int
  category         Category          @relation(fields: [categoryId], references: [id])
  createdAt        DateTime          @default(now())
  transactionItems   TransactionItem[]
  stockItems         StockItem[]
  inventoryAuditItems InventoryAuditItem[]
}

model StockItem {
  id                Int              @id @default(autoincrement())
  productId         Int
  product           Product          @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  serialNumber      String           @unique
  status            String           @default("AVAILABLE") // "AVAILABLE", "SOLD"
  createdAt         DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime         @updatedAt
  transactionItemId Int?
  transactionItem   TransactionItem? @relation(fields: [transactionItemId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
}

model Transaction {
  id              Int               @id @default(autoincrement())
  cashierId       Int
  cashier         User              @relation(fields: [cashierId], references: [id])
  totalAmount     Float
  taxAmount       Float             @default(0)
  discount        Float             @default(0)
  paymentModeId   Int?
  paymentMode     PaymentMode?      @relation(fields: [paymentModeId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  paymentModeName String?
  amountTendered  Float?
  changeAmount    Float?
  status          String            @default("COMPLETED") // "COMPLETED", "VOIDED"
  createdAt       DateTime          @default(now())
  items           TransactionItem[]
}

model TransactionItem {
  id            Int         @id @default(autoincrement())
  transactionId Int
  transaction   Transaction @relation(fields: [transactionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  productId     Int
  product       Product     @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id])
  quantity      Int
  price         Float
  subtotal      Float
  taxAmount     Float       @default(0)
  stockItems    StockItem[]
}

model Settings {
  id              Int     @id @default(1)
  appName         String  @default("Shadow Apollo")
  subtitle        String  @default("Inventory & POS System")
  logoIcon        String  @default("⚡")
  logoUrl         String?
  posPin          String?
  posPinLength    Int     @default(4)
  emailDomain     String  @default("shadow.com")
  maintenanceMode Boolean @default(false)
}

model PaymentMode {
  id           Int           @id @default(autoincrement())
  name         String        @unique
  description  String        @default("")
  isActive     Boolean       @default(true)
  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  transactions Transaction[]
}

model AuditLog {
  id        Int      @id @default(autoincrement())
  userId    Int?
  user      User?    @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  action    String   // e.g., "CREATE", "UPDATE", "DELETE", "LOGIN"
  entity    String   // e.g., "Product", "User", "Transaction"
  entityId  Int?     // The ID of the affected entity
  details   String?  // JSON string containing changes or additional info
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
}

model InventoryAudit {
  id          Int                  @id @default(autoincrement())
  auditorId   Int
  auditor     User                 @relation(fields: [auditorId], references: [id])
  status      String               @default("PENDING") // "PENDING", "COMPLETED", "CANCELLED"
  notes       String?
  createdAt   DateTime             @default(now())
  completedAt DateTime?
  items       InventoryAuditItem[]
}

model InventoryAuditItem {
  id          Int            @id @default(autoincrement())
  auditId     Int
  audit       InventoryAudit @relation(fields: [auditId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  productId   Int
  product     Product        @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  expectedQty Int
  actualQty   Int
  variance    Int            // actualQty - expectedQty
  notes       String?
}

FILE: server\prisma\seed.ts

import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
declare const process: any;

const prisma = new PrismaClient();

async function main() {
    // Seed admin user
    const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash('admin123', 10);
    await prisma.user.upsert({
        where: { email: 'admin@shadow.com' },
        update: {},
        create: {
            name: 'System Admin',
            email: 'admin@shadow.com',
            password: hashedPassword,
            role: 'ADMIN',
        },
    });

    // Seed default settings
    await prisma.settings.upsert({
        where: { id: 1 },
        update: {},
        create: {
            id: 1,
            appName: 'Shadow Apollo',
            subtitle: 'Inventory & POS System',
            logoIcon: '⚡',
            maintenanceMode: false,
        },
    });

    // Seed categories
    const categories = ['Electronics', 'Groceries', 'Beverages', 'Snacks', 'Personal Care', 'Household'];
    for (const name of categories) {
        await prisma.category.upsert({
            where: { name },
            update: {},
            create: { name },
        });
    }

    // Seed default payment modes
    const paymentModes = [
        { name: 'Cash', description: 'Standard physical currency' },
        { name: 'Credit/Debit Card', description: 'Visa, Mastercard, etc.' },
        { name: 'Mobile Wallet', description: 'GCash, Maya, WeChat Pay, etc.' },
        { name: 'Bank Transfer', description: 'Direct bank transfer / wire' }
    ];
    for (const pm of paymentModes) {
        await (prisma as any).paymentMode.upsert({
            where: { name: pm.name },
            update: {},
            create: { name: pm.name, description: pm.description, isActive: true },
        });
    }

    console.log('✅ Database seeded successfully');
    console.log('   Admin: admin@shadow.com / admin123');
}

main()
    .catch((e) => {
        console.error(e);
        process.exit(1);
    })
    .finally(async () => {
        await prisma.$disconnect();
    });
